local function i(j, k, l)
    local m = Instance.new(j)
    for n, o in pairs(k) do
        m[n] = o
    end
    if l then
        for n, o in ipairs(l) do
            o.Parent = m
        end
    end
    return m
end

end)

end

local function u(v)
    if not p then return end
    if activeColorpicker then return end
    q.Text = v
    p.Visible = true

    if r then r:Disconnect() end
    r = e.InputChanged:Connect(function(w)
        if w.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            p.Position = UDim2.new(0, w.Position.X + 12, 0, w.Position.Y - 24)
        end
    end)

    local w = g:GetMouse()
    p.Position = UDim2.new(0, w.X + 12, 0, w.Y - 24)
end

local function v()
    if not p then return end
    p.Visible = false
    if r then
        r:Disconnect()
        r = nil
    end
end

local function w(x, y)
    if not y or y == "" then return end
    x.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        u(y)
    end)
    x.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        v()
    end)
end

local function x(y, z)
    local A, B, C, D

    z.InputBegan:Connect(function(E)
        if E.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or E.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            A = true
            C = E.Position
            D = y.Position

            E.Changed:Connect(function()
                if E.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    A = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    z.InputChanged:Connect(function(E)
        if E.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or E.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            B = E
        end
    end)

    e.InputChanged:Connect(function(E)
        if E == B and A then
            local F = E.Position - C
            y.Position = UDim2.new(
                D.X.Scale, D.X.Offset + F.X,
                D.Y.Scale, D.Y.Offset + F.Y
            )
        end
    end)
end

local D = e.TouchEnabled and not e.KeyboardEnabled

local function F(G, H)
    local I, J, K, L

    H.InputBegan:Connect(function(M)
        if M.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or M.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            I = true
            K = M.Position
            L = G.Size

            M.Changed:Connect(function()
                if M.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    I = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    H.InputChanged:Connect(function(M)
        if M.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or M.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            J = M
        end
    end)

    e.InputChanged:Connect(function(M)
        if M == J and I then
            local N = M.Position - K
            local O = math.max(y, L.X.Offset + N.X)
            local P = math.max(z, L.Y.Offset + N.Y)
            G.Size = UDim2.new(0, O, 0, P)
        end
    end)
end

local function G(H, I, J)
    local K, L, M
    local N = math.floor(H * 6)
    local O = H * 6 - N
    local P = J * (1 - I)
    local Q = J * (1 - O * I)
    local R = J * (1 - (1 - O) * I)
    local S = N % 6
    if S == 0 then K, L, M = J, R, P
    elseif S == 1 then K, L, M = Q, J, P
    elseif S == 2 then K, L, M = P, J, R
    elseif S == 3 then K, L, M = P, Q, J
    elseif S == 4 then K, L, M = R, P, J
    elseif S == 5 then K, L, M = J, P, Q
    end
    return math.floor(K * 255), math.floor(L * 255), math.floor(M * 255)
end

local function H(I, J, K)
    I, J, K = I / 255, J / 255, K / 255
    local L = math.max(I, J, K)
    local M = math.min(I, J, K)
    local N = L - M
    local O, P, Q
    Q = L
    if L == 0 then P = 0 else P = N / L end
    if N == 0 then
        O = 0
    elseif L == I then
        O = ((J - K) / N) % 6
    elseif L == J then
        O = (K - I) / N + 2
    else
        O = (I - J) / N + 4
    end
    O = O / 6
    if O < 0 then O = O + 1 end
    return O, P, Q
end

local function I(J, K, L)
    return string.format("#%02x%02x%02x", J, K, L)
end

local function J(K)
    K = K:gsub("#", "")
    if #K ~= 6 then return nil end
    local L = tonumber(K:sub(1, 2), 16)
    local M = tonumber(K:sub(3, 4), 16)
    local N = tonumber(K:sub(5, 6), 16)
    if L and M and N then return L, M, N end
    return nil
end

local K = nil
local L = nil
local M = nil

local function N()
    if K then
        K.Visible = false
        K = nil
    end
end

local function O()
    if L then
        L.Visible = false
        L = nil
        if M then
            M()
            M = nil
        end
    end
end

        ScrollBarThickness = D and 6 or 3,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = h.Accent,
        ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        HorizontalScrollBarInset = Enum.ScrollBarInset.Always,
        VerticalScrollBarPosition = Enum.VerticalScrollBarPosition.Right,
        Parent = ad.Page,
    })

    i("UIPadding", {
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10),
        Parent = ad._ScrollFrame,
    })

    ad._LeftColumn = i("Frame", {
        Name = "LeftColumn",
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -(h.Padding / 2), 0, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = ad._ScrollFrame,
    })

    i("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, h.Padding),
        Parent = ad._LeftColumn,
    })

    i("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 2),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 2),
        Parent = ad._LeftColumn,
    })

    ad._RightColumn = i("Frame", {
        Name = "RightColumn",
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -(h.Padding / 2), 0, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, h.Padding / 2, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = ad._ScrollFrame,
    })

    i("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, h.Padding),
        Parent = ad._RightColumn,
    })

    i("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 2),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 2),
        Parent = ad._RightColumn,
    })

    ad.Button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not ad.Active then
            ad.Button.TextColor3 = h.TabHover
        end
    end)

    ad.Button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not ad.Active then
            ad.Button.TextColor3 = h.TabInactive
        end
    end)

    ad.Button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        ab:_SelectTab(ad)
    end)

    return ad
end

function aa:SetActive(ab)
    self.Active = ab
    self.Page.Visible = ab
    self.ActiveLine.Visible = ab
    self.Button.TextColor3 = ab and h.TabActive or h.TabInactive
end

local ab = {}
ab.__index = ab

function ab.new(ac, ad, ae)
    local af = setmetatable({}, ab)
    af.Title = ad
    af.Tab = ac

    local ag = (ae == "right") and ac._RightColumn or ac._LeftColumn

    af.Frame = i("Frame", {
        Name = ad .. "_Section",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundColor3 = h.GroupBackground,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ag,
    })
    m(af.Frame, Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 1)

    local ah = i("Frame", {
        Name = "InnerBorder",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.GroupBackground,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ClipsDescendants = false,
        Parent = af.Frame,
    })
    m(ah, Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35), 1)

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Title",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 14),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, -5),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.GroupBackground,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Text = "  " .. string.upper(ad) .. "  ",
        TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSizeSmall,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ZIndex = 3,
        Parent = af.Frame,
    })

    af.Content = i("Frame", {
        Name = "Content",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = ah,
    })

    i("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, h.ItemSpacing),
        Parent = af.Content,
    })

    i("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 12),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
        Parent = af.Content,
    })

    return af
end

function aa:Section(ac, ad)
    return ab.new(self, ac, ad)
end

function ab:Button(ac)
    ac = ac or {}
    local ad = ac.Text or "Button"
    local ae = ac.Func or function() end
    local af = ac.DoubleClick or false
    local ag = ac.Tooltip or nil

    local ah = n(self.Content, ad .. "_ButtonOuter", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 28 or 22))

    local ai = o(ah, "TextButton", {
        Name = "Btn",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28),
        Text = ad,
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
    })

    local aj = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28)
    local ak = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
    local al = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22)

    ai.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        ai.BackgroundColor3 = ak
    end)

    ai.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        ai.BackgroundColor3 = aj
    end)

    if af then
        local am = 0
        ai.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            ai.BackgroundColor3 = al
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                ai.BackgroundColor3 = ak
            end)

            local an = tick()
            if an - am < 0.4 then
                ae()
                am = 0
            else
                am = an
            end
        end)
    else
        ai.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            ai.BackgroundColor3 = al
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                ai.BackgroundColor3 = ak
            end)
            ae()
        end)
    end
    w(ai, ag)

    return ai
end

function ab:Toggle(ac, ad)
    ad = ad or {}
    local ae = ad.Text or ac
    local af = ad.Default or false
    local ag = ad.Tooltip or nil
    local ah = ad.Callback or function() end
    local ai = af

    local aj = i("Frame", {
        Name = ac .. "_Toggle",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 28 or 22),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = self.Content,
    })

    local ak, al = n(aj, "CheckOuter", D and UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18) or UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, D and -9 or -7), { OuterClass = "TextButton", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false })

    local am = o(ak, "Frame", {
        Name = "Fill",
        BackgroundColor3 = ai and h.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
    })

    local an = i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Label",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = ae,
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = aj,
    })

    local function ao()
        am.BackgroundColor3 = ai and h.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
    end

    local function ap()
        ai = not ai
        ao()
        ah(ai)
    end

    al.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ap)

    local aq = i("TextButton", {
        Name = "LabelBtn",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Parent = aj,
    })
    aq.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ap)
    w(aj, ag)

    local ar = {
        Instance = aj,
        SetValue = function(ar, as)
            ai = as
            l(ao)
            task.spawn(ah, ai)
        end,
        GetValue = function()
            return ai
        end,
    }

    local as = self.Tab.Window

    function ar:AddColorpicker(at, au)
        local av = aj
        while av and not av:IsA("ScreenGui") do
            av = av.Parent
        end
        return P(at, au, aj, an, av, as and as._registry)
    end

    function ar:AddKeybind(at, au)
        au = au or {}
        local av = au.Default or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
        local aw = au.Mode or "Toggle"
        local ax = au.ChangedCallback or function() end

        local ay = av
        local az = false

        an.Size = UDim2.new(an.Size.X.Scale, an.Size.X.Offset - 34, an.Size.Y.Scale, an.Size.Y.Offset)

        local aA = n(aj, "KBOuter", UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 14), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0.5, -7))

        local aB = o(aA, "TextButton", {
            Name = "KBBtn",
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28),
            Text = ay == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "..." or ay.Name,
            TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary,
            FontFace = h.Font,
            TextSize = 9,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        })

        aB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            az = true
            aB.Text = "..."
            aB.TextColor3 = h.Accent
        end)

        e.InputBegan:Connect(function(aC, aD)
            if az then
                if aC.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
                    aB.Text = ay == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "..." or ay.Name
                    aB.TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary
                    az = false
                    return
                end
                if aC.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                    ay = aC.KeyCode
                elseif aC.UserInputType.Name:match("^MouseButton%d+$") then
                    ay = aC.UserInputType
                else
                    return
                end
                aB.Text = ay.Name
                aB.TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary
                az = false
                ax(ay)
                return
            end
            if not aD and (aC.KeyCode == ay or aC.UserInputType == ay) and ay ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                if aw == "Toggle" then
                    ar:SetValue(not ar:GetValue())
                end
            end
        end)

        e.InputEnded:Connect(function(aC)
            if not az and (aC.KeyCode == ay or aC.UserInputType == ay) and ay ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                if aw == "Hold" then
                    ar:SetValue(false)
                end
            end
        end)

        if aw == "Hold" then
            e.InputBegan:Connect(function(aC, aD)
                if not aD and not az and (aC.KeyCode == ay or aC.UserInputType == ay) and ay ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                    ar:SetValue(true)
                end
            end)
        end

        local aC = {
            Instance = aj,
            SetValue = function(aC, aD)
                ay = aD
                l(function()
                    aB.Text = aD == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "..." or aD.Name
                    aB.TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary
                end)
                task.spawn(ax, aD)
            end,
            GetValue = function()
                return ay
            end,
        }

        if as and as._registry then
            as._registry[at] = aC
        end

        if as and as._keybinds then
            table.insert(as._keybinds, { toggle = ar, keybind = aC, text = ae })
        end

        return ar
    end

    local at = self.Tab.Window
    if at._registry then
        at._registry[ac] = ar
    end

    return ar
end

function ab:AddInput(ac, ad)
    ad = ad or {}
    local ae = ad.Text or ac
    local af = ad.Default or ""
    local ag = ad.Numeric or false
    local ah = ad.Finished or false
    local ai = ad.Tooltip or nil
    local aj = ad.Placeholder or ""
    local ak = ad.MaxLength or nil
    local al = ad.Callback or function() end

    local am = i("Frame", {
        Name = ac .. "_Input",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 46 or 38),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = self.Content,
    })

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Label",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = ae,
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = am,
    })

    local an, ao = n(am, "InputOuter", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 28 or 22), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16))

    local ap = o(an, "TextBox", {
        Name = "TextBox",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
        Text = af,
        PlaceholderText = aj,
        PlaceholderColor3 = h.TextDisabled,
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ClearTextOnFocus = false,
    })

    i("UIPadding", {
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4),
        Parent = ap,
    })

    ap.Focused:Connect(function()
        ao.BackgroundColor3 = h.Accent
    end)

    ap.FocusLost:Connect(function(aq)
        ao.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)

        local ar = ap.Text

        if ak and #ar > ak then
            ar = string.sub(ar, 1, ak)
            ap.Text = ar
        end

        if ag then
            ar = ar:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "")
            ap.Text = ar
        end

        if ah then
            if aq then
                al(ar)
            end
        else
            al(ar)
        end
    end)

    if not ah then
        ap:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            local aq = ap.Text

            if ak and #aq > ak then
                aq = string.sub(aq, 1, ak)
                ap.Text = aq
            end

            if ag then
                local ar = aq:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "")
                if ar ~= aq then
                    ap.Text = ar
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    w(am, ai)

    local aq = {
        Instance = am,
        SetValue = function(aq, ar)
            local as = tostring(ar)
            l(function() ap.Text = as end)
            pcall(al, as)
        end,
        GetValue = function()
            return ap.Text
        end,
    }

    local ar = self.Tab.Window
    if ar._registry then
        ar._registry[ac] = aq
    end

    return aq
end

function ab:AddSlider(ac, ad)
    ad = ad or {}
    local ae = ad.Text or ac
    local af = ad.Default or 0
    local ag = ad.Min or 0
    local ah = ad.Max or 100
    local ai = ad.Rounding or 0
    local aj = ad.Tooltip or nil
    local ak = ad.Callback or function() end

    local al = math.clamp(af, ag, ah)
    local am = i("Frame", {
        Name = ac .. "_Slider",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 42 or 36),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = self.Content,
    })

    local an = i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Label",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 14),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = ae,
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = am,
    })

    local ao = i("TextBox", {
        Name = "ValueBox",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 14),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Text = tostring(al),
        TextColor3 = h.Accent,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        ClearTextOnFocus = true,
        Parent = am,
    })

    local ap, aq = n(am, "TrackOuter", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 20 or 14), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 18))

    local ar = o(ap, "Frame", {
        Name = "TrackBg",
        ClipsDescendants = true,
    })

    local as = i("Frame", {
        Name = "Fill",
        Size = UDim2.new((al - ag) / (ah - ag), 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.Accent,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ar,
    })

    local at = i("TextButton", {
        Name = "ClickArea",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Parent = aq,
    })

    local function au(av)
        if ai == 0 then
            return math.floor(av + 0.5)
        else
            local aw = 10 ^ ai
            return math.floor(av * aw + 0.5) / aw
        end
    end

    local function av(aw)
        al = au(math.clamp(aw, ag, ah))
        local ax = (al - ag) / (ah - ag)
        as.Size = UDim2.new(ax, 0, 1, 0)
        ao.Text = tostring(al)
    end

    ao.FocusLost:Connect(function(aw)
        local ax = ao.Text:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "")
        local ay = tonumber(ax)
        if ay and ay >= ag and ay <= ah then
            av(ay)
            ak(al)
        else
            av(af)
            ak(al)
        end
    end)

    ao:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        local aw = ao.Text:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "")
        if aw ~= ao.Text then
            ao.Text = aw
        end
    end)

    local aw = false
    at.InputBegan:Connect(function(ax)
        if ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            aw = true
            local ay = math.clamp((ax.Position.X - ar.AbsolutePosition.X) / ar.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            local az = ag + (ah - ag) * ay
            av(az)
            ak(al)
        end
    end)

    at.InputEnded:Connect(function(ax)
        if ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            aw = false
        end
    end)

    e.InputChanged:Connect(function(ax)
        if aw and (ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local ay = math.clamp((ax.Position.X - ar.AbsolutePosition.X) / ar.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            local az = ag + (ah - ag) * ay
            av(az)
            ak(al)
        end
    end)

    e.InputEnded:Connect(function(ax)
        if ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            aw = false
        end
    end)
    w(am, aj)

    av(al)
    local ax = {
        Instance = am,
        SetValue = function(ax, ay)
            l(function() av(ay) end)
            task.spawn(ak, al)
        end,
        GetValue = function()
            return al
        end,
    }

    local ay = self.Tab.Window
    if ay._registry then
        ay._registry[ac] = ax
    end

    return ax
end

function ab:Label(ac)
    local ad = i("Frame", {
        Name = "Label_" .. ac,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = self.Content,
    })

    local ae = i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Text",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = ac,
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = ad,
    })

    local af = {
        Instance = ad,
        SetText = function(af, ag)
            local ah = tostring(ag)
            l(function() ae.Text = ah end)
        end,
    }

    local ag = self.Tab.Window

    function af:AddColorpicker(ah, ai)
        local aj = ad
        while aj and not aj:IsA("ScreenGui") do
            aj = aj.Parent
        end
        return P(ah, ai, ad, ae, aj, ag and ag._registry)
    end

    return af
end

function ab:List(ac)
    ac = ac or {}
    local ad = ac.Values or ac.Items or {}
    local ae = ac.MaxVisible or 6
    local af = ac.OnClick or nil
    local ag = ac.FormatText or tostring

    local ah = 22
    local ai = math.min(#ad, ae) * ah

    local aj = i("Frame", {
        Name = "List",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ai),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Parent = self.Content,
    })

    m(aj, Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40), 1)
    local ak = i("ScrollingFrame", {
        Name = "Scroll",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ScrollBarThickness = 2,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = h.Accent,
        ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.3,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #ad * ah),
        Parent = aj,
    })

    i("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 0),
        Parent = ak,
    })

    local al = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22)
    local am = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
    local an = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
    local ao = {}

    local function ap()
        for aq, ar in ipairs(ao) do
            ar:Destroy()
        end

        ao = {}
        ak.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #ad * ah)
        aj.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.min(#ad, ae) * ah)

        for aq, ar in ipairs(ad) do
            local as = (aq % 2 == 1) and al or am

            local at = i("Frame", {
                Name = "Row_" .. aq,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ah),
                BackgroundColor3 = as,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                LayoutOrder = aq,
                Parent = ak,
            })

            if aq < #ad then
                i("Frame", {
                    Name = "Sep",
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1),
                    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Parent = at,
                })
            end

            i("TextLabel", {
                Name = "Text",
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = ag(ar),
                TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
                FontFace = h.Font,
                TextSize = h.FontSize,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Parent = at,
            })

            if af then
                local au = i("TextButton", {
                    Name = "Hit",
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Text = "",
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    Parent = at,
                })

                au.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    at.BackgroundColor3 = an
                end)

                au.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    at.BackgroundColor3 = as
                end)

                au.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    af(aq, ar)
                end)
            end

            ao[aq] = at
        end
    end

    ap()

    local aq = {
        Instance = aj,
        SetValues = function(aq, ar)
            ad = ar
            ap()
        end,
        GetValues = function()
            return ad
        end,
    }

    return aq
end

function ab:AddDropdown(ac, ad)
    ad = ad or {}
    local ae = ad.Values or {}
    local af = ad.Multi or false
    local ag = ad.Text or ac
    local ah = ad.Tooltip or nil
    local ai = ad.Callback or function() end

    local aj
    local ak = nil
    if af then
        aj = {}
        if type(ad.Default) == "table" then
            for al, am in ipairs(ad.Default) do
                aj[am] = true
            end
        end
    else
        if type(ad.Default) == "number" then
            aj = ae[ad.Default] or ae[1]
            ak = ad.Default
        elseif type(ad.Default) == "string" then
            aj = ad.Default
            for al, am in ipairs(ae) do
                if am == ad.Default then ak = al; break end
            end
        else
            aj = ae[1] or ""
            ak = 1
        end
    end

    local al = false
    local am = self.Tab.Window._ScreenGui
    local an = i("Frame", {
        Name = ac .. "_Dropdown",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 46 or 38),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        ClipsDescendants = false,
        Parent = self.Content,
    })

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Label",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = ag,
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = an,
    })

    local function ao()
        if af then
            local ap = {}
            for aq, ar in ipairs(ae) do
                if aj[ar] then
                    table.insert(ap, ar)
                end
            end
            if #ap == 0 then return "..." end
            return table.concat(ap, ", ")
        else
            return aj or "..."
        end
    end

    local ap, aq = n(an, "BtnOuter", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 28 or 22), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16))

    local ar = o(ap, "TextButton", {
        Name = "Face",
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
    })

    local as = i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Display",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = ao(),
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        Parent = ar,
    })

    local at = i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Arrow",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "v",
        TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary,
        FontFace = h.FontBold,
        TextSize = 10,
        Parent = ar,
    })

    local au, av = n(am, ac .. "_DropList", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), nil, {
        Class = "ScrollingFrame",
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 100,
        Inner = {
            Name = "Inner",
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            ScrollBarThickness = 3,
            ScrollBarImageColor3 = h.Accent,
            ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.3,
            CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        },
    })

    local aw = i("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 0),
        Parent = au,
    })

    local function ax()
        local ay = aq.AbsolutePosition
        local az = aq.AbsoluteSize
        local aA = D and 28 or 22
        local aB = 6
        local aC = math.min(#ae, aB)
        av.Position = UDim2.new(0, ay.X, 0, ay.Y + az.Y + 2)
        av.Size = UDim2.new(0, az.X, 0, aC * aA + 6)
    end

    self.Tab._ScrollFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
        if al then
            ax()
        end
    end)

    local ay = {}
    local function az()
        as.Text = ao()
    end

    local function aA()
        for aB, aC in ipairs(ay) do
            aC:Destroy()
        end
        ay = {}

        for aB, aC in ipairs(ae) do
            local aD
            if af then
                aD = aj[aC]
            else
                aD = (aB == ak)
            end

            local aE = i("TextButton", {
                Name = "Opt_" .. aB .. "_" .. aC,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 28 or 22),
                BackgroundColor3 = aD and Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28) or Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                ZIndex = 101,
                Parent = au,
            })

            local aF = i("TextLabel", {
                Name = "Label",
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, af and 24 or 6, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = aC,
                TextColor3 = aD and h.Accent or h.TextPrimary,
                FontFace = h.Font,
                TextSize = h.FontSize,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                ZIndex = 101,
                Parent = aE,
            })

            if af then
                local aG = n(aE, "ChkOuter", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12), UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, -6), { ZIndex = 101 })

                o(aG, "Frame", {
                    Name = "Fill",
                    ZIndex = 101,
                    BackgroundColor3 = aD and h.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
                })
            end

            aE.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                aE.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 32)
            end)

            aE.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                local aG = af and aj[aC] or (aB == ak)
                aE.BackgroundColor3 = aG and Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28) or Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
            end)

            aE.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if af then
                    aj[aC] = not aj[aC]
                    aA()
                    az()
                    local aG = {}
                    for aH, aI in ipairs(ae) do
                        if aj[aI] then
                            table.insert(aG, aI)
                        end
                    end
                    ai(aG)
                else
                    aj = aC
                    ak = aB
                    az()
                    ai(aC)
                    al = false
                    av.Visible = false
                    at.Text = "v"
                end
            end)

            table.insert(ay, aE)
        end
    end

    aA()

    local function aB()
        al = false
        av.Visible = false
        at.Text = "v"
    end

    ar.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if al then
            aB()
            L = nil
            M = nil
        else
            O()
            N()
            al = true
            ax()
            aA()
            av.Visible = true
            at.Text = "^"
            L = av
            M = aB
        end
    end)

    e.InputBegan:Connect(function(aC)
        if not al then return end
        if aC.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            local aD = aC.Position
            local aE = av.AbsolutePosition
            local aF = av.AbsoluteSize
            local aG = aq.AbsolutePosition
            local aH = aq.AbsoluteSize

            local aI = aD.X >= aE.X and aD.X <= aE.X + aF.X
                and aD.Y >= aE.Y and aD.Y <= aE.Y + aF.Y
            local aJ = aD.X >= aG.X and aD.X <= aG.X + aH.X
                and aD.Y >= aG.Y and aD.Y <= aG.Y + aH.Y

            if not aI and not aJ then
                aB()
                if L == av then
                    L = nil
                    M = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    ar.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        ar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22)
    end)

    ar.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        ar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
    end)
    w(an, ah)

    local aC = {
        Instance = an,
        SetValue = function(aC, aD)
            if af then
                aj = {}
                if type(aD) == "table" then
                    for aE, aF in ipairs(aD) do
                        aj[aF] = true
                    end
                end
            else
                aj = aD
                ak = nil
                for aE, aF in ipairs(ae) do
                    if aF == aD then ak = aE; break end
                end
            end
            l(az)
            l(aA)
            task.spawn(ai, aD)
        end,
        GetValue = function()
            if af then
                local aC = {}
                for aD, aE in ipairs(ae) do
                    if aj[aE] then
                        table.insert(aC, aE)
                    end
                end
                return aC
            end
            return aj
        end,
        SetValues = function(aC, aD)
            ae = aD
            if af then
                local aE = {}
                for aF, aG in ipairs(ae) do
                    if aj[aG] then
                        aE[aG] = true
                    end
                end
                aj = aE
            else
                local aE = false
                for aF, aG in ipairs(ae) do
                    if aG == aj then aE = true; ak = aF; break end
                end
                if not aE then
                    aj = ae[1] or ""
                    ak = 1
                end
            end
            l(az)
            if al then
                l(aA)
                l(ax)
            end
        end,
    }

    local aD = self.Tab.Window
    if aD._registry then
        aD._registry[ac] = aC
    end

    return aC
end

local ac = {}
ac.__index = ac

function ac.new(ad)
    local ae = setmetatable({}, ac)
    ae.Title = ad
    ae.Tabs = {}
    ae.ActiveTab = nil
    ae.Visible = true
    ae._registry = {}
    ae._keybinds = {}
    ae._toggleKey = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift

    local af = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789"
    local ag = ""
    for ah = 1, 16 do
        local ai = math.random(1, #af)
        ag = ag .. af:sub(ai, ai)
    end

    local ah = s()
    if ah then
        for ai, aj in ipairs(ah:GetChildren()) do
            if aj:IsA("ScreenGui") and aj:GetAttribute("_UILib") then
                aj:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    ae._ScreenGui = i("ScreenGui", {
        Name = ag,
        ResetOnSpawn = false,
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        Parent = ah,
    })
    ae._ScreenGui:SetAttribute("_UILib", true)
    t(ae._ScreenGui)

    local ai = math.min(759, workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.X - 20)
    local aj = math.min(540, workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.Y - 60)

    ae._OuterBorder = i("Frame", {
        Name = "OuterBorder",
        Size = E
            and UDim2.new(0, ai, 0, aj)
            or UDim2.new(0, 650, 0, 450),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, E and -ai / 2 or -225, 0.5, E and -aj / 2 or -150),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.WindowBackground,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ae._ScreenGui,
    })
    m(ae._OuterBorder, h.WindowBorder, 1)

    ae._InnerFrame = i("Frame", {
        Name = "InnerFrame",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.WindowBackground,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ae._OuterBorder,
    })
    m(ae._InnerFrame, Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40), 1)

    ae._Header = i("Frame", {
        Name = "Header",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 32 or h.HeaderHeight),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.WindowHeader,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ae._InnerFrame,
    })

    local ak = string.format("#%02x%02x%02x", math.floor(h.Accent.R * 255), math.floor(h.Accent.G * 255), math.floor(h.Accent.B * 255))
    local al = string.upper(ad)
    local am
    local an = al:find("%.[^%.]*$")
    if an then
        local ao = al:sub(1, an - 1)
        local ap = al:sub(an)
        am = '<font color="#ffffff">' .. ao .. '</font><font color="' .. ak .. '">' .. ap .. '</font>'
    else
        am = '<font color="' .. ak .. '">' .. al .. '</font>'
    end

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Title",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        RichText = true,
        Text = am,
        TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        FontFace = h.FontBold,
        TextSize = h.FontSizeTitle,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = ae._Header,
    })

    i("Frame", {
        Name = "HeaderSep",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.GroupBorder,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ae._Header,
    })

    x(ae._OuterBorder, ae._Header)

    ae._TabBar = i("Frame", {
        Name = "TabBar",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, D and 34 or h.TabHeight),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (D and 32 or h.HeaderHeight) + 1),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.TabBarBackground,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ae._InnerFrame,
    })

    ae._TabContainer = i("Frame", {
        Name = "TabContainer",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = ae._TabBar,
    })

    i("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 0),
        Parent = ae._TabContainer,
    })

    i("Frame", {
        Name = "TabSep",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.GroupBorder,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ae._TabBar,
    })

    local ao = (D and 32 or h.HeaderHeight) + 1 + (D and 34 or h.TabHeight) + 1
    ae._ContentFrame = i("Frame", {
        Name = "Content",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -ao - 18),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, ao),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.ContentBackground,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Parent = ae._InnerFrame,
    })

    ae._ResizeGrip = i("TextButton", {
        Name = "ResizeGrip",
        Size = D and UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24) or UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14),
        Position = D and UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, -24) or UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -14),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 5,
        Parent = ae._OuterBorder,
    })

    for ap = 1, 3 do
        i("Frame", {
            Name = "GripLine" .. ap,
            Size = UDim2.new(0, ap * 4, 0, 1),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -(ap * 4) - 1, 1, -(ap * 3) - 1),
            BackgroundColor3 = h.Accent,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = ae._ResizeGrip,
        })
    end

    F(ae._OuterBorder, ae._ResizeGrip)
    ae._Footer = i("Frame", {
        Name = "Footer",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -18),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 16),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ae._InnerFrame,
    })

    i("Frame", {
        Name = "FooterSep",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.GroupBorder,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ae._Footer,
    })

    local ap = string.format("#%02x%02x%02x", math.floor(h.Accent.R * 255), math.floor(h.Accent.G * 255), math.floor(h.Accent.B * 255))
    local aq = string.upper(ad)
    local ar
    local as = aq:find("%.[^%.]*$")
    if as then
        ar = '<font color="#ffffff">' .. aq:sub(1, as - 1) .. '</font><font color="' .. ap .. '">' .. aq:sub(as) .. '</font>'
    else
        ar = '<font color="' .. ap .. '">' .. aq .. '</font>'
    end
    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "FooterLeft",
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        RichText = true,
        Text = ar,
        TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSizeSmall,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = ae._Footer,
    })

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "FooterRight",
        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -20, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "v1.0",
        TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSizeSmall,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        Parent = ae._Footer,
    })

    ae._cursor_saved = nil

    local function at(au)
        if ae._destroyed then return end
        if ae.Visible then
            if au and ae._cursor_saved == nil then
                ae._cursor_saved = e.MouseIconEnabled
            end
            if not e.MouseIconEnabled then
                e.MouseIconEnabled = true
            end
        elseif ae._cursor_saved ~= nil then
            e.MouseIconEnabled = ae._cursor_saved
            ae._cursor_saved = nil
        end
    end

    ae._sync_cursor = at
    ae._cursor_conn = e:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MouseIconEnabled"):Connect(function()
        at(false)
    end)

    at(false)

    e.InputBegan:Connect(function(au, av)
        if av then return end
        if not D and (au.KeyCode == ae._toggleKey or au.UserInputType == ae._toggleKey) then
            ae.Visible = not ae.Visible
            ae._OuterBorder.Visible = ae.Visible
            O()
            N()
            at(ae.Visible)
        end
    end)

    if D then
        local au = i("TextButton", {
            Name = "CloseBtn",
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 2),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "X",
            TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary,
            FontFace = h.FontBold,
            TextSize = 12,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            ZIndex = 10,
            Parent = ae._Header,
        })
        au.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            ae.Visible = false
            ae._OuterBorder.Visible = false
            O()
            N()
            at()
        end)
    end

    return ae
end

function ac:Tab(ad)
    local ae = aa.new(self, ad)
    table.insert(self.Tabs, ae)
    if #self.Tabs == 1 then
        self:_SelectTab(ae)
    end

    return ae
end

function ac:_SelectTab(ad)
    if self.ActiveTab then
        self.ActiveTab:SetActive(false)
    end
    self.ActiveTab = ad
    ad:SetActive(true)
end

function ac:Destroy()
    self._destroyed = true
    if self._cursor_conn then
        self._cursor_conn:Disconnect()
        self._cursor_conn = nil
    end
    if self._cursor_saved ~= nil then
        e.MouseIconEnabled = self._cursor_saved
        self._cursor_saved = nil
    end
    if self._registry then
        for ad, ae in self._registry do
            if ae.GetValue and ae.SetValue then
                local af, ag = pcall(ae.GetValue)
                if af and type(ag) == "boolean" and ag == true then
                    pcall(ae.SetValue, ae, false)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if self._onDestroy then
        for ad, ae in self._onDestroy do
            pcall(ae)
        end
    end
    self._ScreenGui:Destroy()
    if self._watermark then
        self._watermark:Destroy()
    end
end

function ac:OnDestroy(ad)
    if not self._onDestroy then self._onDestroy = {} end
    table.insert(self._onDestroy, ad)
end

function ac:Register(ad, ae)
    self._registry[ad] = ae
    return ae
end

function ac:Watermark(ad)
    ad = ad or {}
    local ae = ad.GameName ~= false
    local af = ad.Players ~= false
    local ag = ad.FPS ~= false
    local ah = ad.TimeLeft ~= false

    local ai = game:GetService("RunService")

    local aj, ak = n(self._ScreenGui, "Watermark", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10))
    ak.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X

    local al = o(aj, "Frame", {
        Name = "Face",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 14),
    })

    local am = i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Text",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "",
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = al,
    })

    i("UIPadding", {
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6),
        Parent = al,
    })

    local an = 0
    local ao = 0
    local ap = tick()
    local aq = false

    ai.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if self._destroyed then return end
        ao = ao + 1
        local ar = tick()
        if ar - ap >= 1 then
            an = ao
            ao = 0
            ap = ar
        end

        local as = {}

        if ae then
            as[#as + 1] = self.Title
        end

        if af then
            as[#as + 1] = tostring(#c:GetPlayers()) .. " players"
        end

        if ag then
            as[#as + 1] = tostring(an) .. " fps"
        end

        if ah then
            local at = tonumber(workspace:GetAttribute("celsius_clp_time_left")) or 0
            local au
            if at < 0 then
                au = "Free"
            elseif at >= 31536000 then
                au = "Lifetime"
            else
                local av = math.floor(at / 86400)
                local aw = math.floor(at % 86400 / 3600)
                local ax = math.floor(at % 3600 / 60)
                local ay = {}
                if av > 0 then
                    ay[#ay + 1] = av .. "d"
                end
                if aw > 0 then
                    ay[#ay + 1] = aw .. "h"
                end
                ay[#ay + 1] = ax .. "m"
                au = table.concat(ay, " ")
            end
            as[#as + 1] = "Time Left: " .. au
        end

        if E and not aq then
            am.Text = "Click to toggle me!"
        else
            am.Text = table.concat(as, " | ")
        end
    end)

    if E then
        local ar = i("TextButton", {
            Name = "Toggle",
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = ak,
        })

        ar.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            aq = true
            self.Visible = not self.Visible
            self._OuterBorder.Visible = self.Visible
            O()
            N()
            self._sync_cursor(self.Visible)
        end)
    end

    self._watermark = ak
    return ak
end

function ac:KeybindList(ad)
    ad = ad or {}
    local ae = ad.Title or "Keybinds"
    local af = 16
    local ag = ad.Width or 170

    local ah, ai = n(self._ScreenGui, "KeybindList", UDim2.new(0, ag, 0, 26), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 46), { Visible = false })

    local aj = o(ah, "Frame", {
        Name = "Face",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 14),
    })

    i("Frame", {
        Name = "AccentLine",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.Accent,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = aj,
    })

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Title",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 16),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 4),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = string.upper(ae),
        TextColor3 = h.Accent,
        FontFace = h.FontBold,
        TextSize = h.FontSizeSmall,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = aj,
    })

    local ak = {}
    local al = nil

    local am = function()
        local am = {}
        local an = {}
        for ao, ap in ipairs(self._keybinds) do
            local aq = ap.keybind:GetValue()
            if aq ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                am[#am + 1] = ap
                an[#an + 1] = tostring(ao) .. aq.Name
            end
        end

        local ao = table.concat(an, ",")
        if ao ~= al then
            al = ao
            for ap, aq in ipairs(ak) do
                aq.frame:Destroy()
            end
            ak = {}
            for ap, aq in ipairs(am) do
                local ar = i("Frame", {
                    Name = "Bind_" .. ap,
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, af),
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 22 + (ap - 1) * af),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Parent = aj,
                })
                ak[ap] = {
                    frame = ar,
                    label = i("TextLabel", {
                        Name = "Label",
                        Size = UDim2.new(1, -54, 1, 0),
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                        Text = aq.text,
                        TextColor3 = h.TextDisabled,
                        FontFace = h.Font,
                        TextSize = h.FontSizeSmall,
                        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                        Parent = ar,
                    }),
                    key = i("TextLabel", {
                        Name = "Key",
                        Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 1, 0),
                        Position = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 0),
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                        TextColor3 = h.TextDisabled,
                        FontFace = h.Font,
                        TextSize = h.FontSizeSmall,
                        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                        Parent = ar,
                    }),
                    key_name = nil,
                    active = nil,
                }
            end
            ai.Size = UDim2.new(0, ag, 0, 28 + #am * af)
        end

        for ap, aq in ipairs(am) do
            local ar = ak[ap]
            local as = aq.keybind:GetValue()
            local at = as == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "-" or as.Name
            if ar.key_name ~= at then
                ar.key_name = at
                ar.key.Text = at
            end
            local au = aq.toggle:GetValue() and true or false
            if ar.active ~= au then
                ar.active = au
                local av = au and h.Accent or h.TextDisabled
                ar.label.TextColor3 = av
                ar.key.TextColor3 = av
            end
        end
    end

    local an, ao, ap

    ai.Active = true
    ai.InputBegan:Connect(function(aq)
        if aq.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and aq.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        an = true
        ao = aq.Position
        ap = ai.AbsolutePosition
        aq.Changed:Connect(function()
            if aq.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                an = false
            end
        end)
    end)

    e.InputChanged:Connect(function(aq)
        if self._destroyed or not an then return end
        if aq.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and aq.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local ar = self._ScreenGui.AbsolutePosition
        local as = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local at = as and as.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
        local au = ai.AbsoluteSize
        local av = aq.Position - ao
        local aw = math.clamp(ap.X + av.X, ar.X, math.max(ar.X, ar.X + at.X - au.X))
        local ax = math.clamp(ap.Y + av.Y, ar.Y, math.max(ar.Y, ar.Y + at.Y - au.Y))
        ai.Position = UDim2.new(0, aw - ar.X, 0, ax - ar.Y)
    end)

    local aq = game:GetService("RunService")
    aq.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if self._destroyed then return end
        if ai.Visible then
            am()
        end
    end)

    return {
        Instance = ai,
        SetVisible = function(ar, as)
            ai.Visible = as
            if as then
                am()
            end
        end,
    }
end

local ad = "celsius/configs/" .. tostring(game.GameId)

local function ae(af)
    if not isfolder(af) then
        makefolder(af)
    end
end

local function af(ag, ah)
    writefile(ag, f:JSONEncode(ah))
end

local function ag(ah)
    if isfile(ah) then
        local ai, aj = pcall(function()
            return f:JSONDecode(readfile(ah))
        end)
        if ai then return aj end
    end
    return nil
end

local function ah(ai)
    if not isfolder(ai) then return {} end
    local aj = {}
    local ak, al = pcall(listfiles, ai)
    if not ak then return {} end
    for am, an in ipairs(al) do
        local ao = tostring(an):match("([^/\\]+)$")
        if ao and ao:match("%.json$") and ao ~= "_autoload.json" then
            aj[#aj + 1] = ao:gsub("%.json$", "")
        end
    end
    return aj
end

local function ai(aj, ak)
    if ak.type == "color" then
        return aj:SetValue(Color3.fromRGB(ak.value[1], ak.value[2], ak.value[3]))
    end

    if ak.type == "keybind" then
        if typeof(ak.value) == "string" then
            local al = Enum.KeyCode[ak.value] or Enum.UserInputType[ak.value]
            if al then
                return aj:SetValue(al)
            end
        end

        return false
    end

    return aj:SetValue(ak.value)
end

function ac:Settings(aj)
    local ak = self:Tab("Settings")
    local al = ak:Section("Menu", "left")

    if aj then
        aj(al)
    end

    al:AddKeybind("MenuBind", {
        Default = self._toggleKey,
        Text = "Menu Bind",
        ChangedCallback = function(am)
            self._toggleKey = am
        end,
    })

    al:AddKeybind("PanicBind", {
        Default = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl,
        Text = "Panic Bind",
        Callback = function(am)
            self:Destroy()
        end,
    })

    al:Button({
        Text = "Unload",
        Func = function()
            self:Destroy()
        end,
    })

    local am = ak:Section("Configs", "left")
    ae("celsius")
    ae("celsius/configs")
    ae(ad)

    local an = am:AddInput("_settings_ConfigName", {
        Text = "Config Name",
        Default = "",
        Placeholder = "my-config",
        Callback = function() end,
    })

    local ao = ah(ad)
    local ap = am:AddDropdown("_settings_ConfigList", {
        Values = ao,
        Default = 1,
        Text = "Config List",
        Callback = function() end,
    })

    local function aq()
        local ar = ah(ad)
        ap:SetValues(ar)
    end

    am:Button({
        Text = "Save",
        Func = function()
            local ar = an:GetValue()
            local as = ap:GetValue()
            local at
            if ar ~= "" then
                at = ar
            elseif as and as ~= "" then
                at = as
            else
                b.Notification("Config", "Enter a name or select a config")
                return
            end

            local au = {}
            if self._registry then
                for av, aw in pairs(self._registry) do
                    if not av:match("^_settings_") and aw.GetValue then
                        local ax = aw:GetValue()
                        if typeof(ax) == "Color3" then
                            au[av] = { type = "color", value = {math.floor(ax.R * 255), math.floor(ax.G * 255), math.floor(ax.B * 255)} }
                        elseif typeof(ax) == "EnumItem" then
                            au[av] = { type = "keybind", value = ax.Name }
                        elseif type(ax) == "boolean" then
                            au[av] = { type = "bool", value = ax }
                        elseif type(ax) == "number" then
                            au[av] = { type = "number", value = ax }
                        elseif type(ax) == "string" then
                            au[av] = { type = "string", value = ax }
                        elseif type(ax) == "table" then
                            au[av] = { type = "table", value = ax }
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            af(ad .. "/" .. at .. ".json", au)
            if not isfile(ad .. "/" .. at .. ".json") then
                b.Notification("Config", "Failed to save " .. at)
                return
            end
            aq()
            b.Notification("Config", at .. " saved")
        end,
    })

    am:Button({
        Text = "Load",
        Func = function()
            local ar = ap:GetValue()
            if not ar or ar == "" then
                b.Notification("Config", "No config selected")
                return
            end

            local as = ag(ad .. "/" .. ar .. ".json")
            if not as then
                b.Notification("Config", "Failed to read " .. ar)
                return
            end

            local at = 0
            if self._registry then
                for au, av in pairs(as) do
                    local aw = self._registry[au]
                    if aw and aw.SetValue then
                        local ax, ay = pcall(ai, aw, av)
                        if not ax then
                            at = at + 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if self._registry["AccentColor"] then
                pcall(function()
                    self._registry["AccentColor"]:SetValue(self._registry["AccentColor"]:GetValue())
                end)
            end

            b.Notification("Config", ar .. " loaded" .. (at > 0 and (", " .. at .. " failed") or ""))
        end,
    })

    am:Button({
        Text = "Delete",
        Func = function()
            local ar = ap:GetValue()
            if not ar or ar == "" then
                b.Notification("Config", "No config selected")
                return
            end

            local as = ad .. "/" .. ar .. ".json"
            pcall(delfile, as)
            if isfile(as) then
                b.Notification("Config", "Failed to delete " .. ar)
                return
            end

            local at = ag(ad .. "/_autoload.json")
            if at and at.autoload == ar then
                pcall(delfile, ad .. "/_autoload.json")
            end

            aq()
            b.Notification("Config", ar .. " deleted")
        end,
    })

    am:Button({
        Text = "Refresh",
        Func = function()
            aq()
        end,
    })

    am:Button({
        Text = "Set as Autoload",
        Func = function()
            local ar = ap:GetValue()
            if ar and ar ~= "" then
                af(ad .. "/_autoload.json", { autoload = ar })
            end
        end,
    })

    local ar = ak:Section("Themes", "right")
    ae("celsius/themes")

    local as = ar:Label("Background Color")
    local at = as:AddColorpicker("BgColor", {
        Default = h.WindowBackground,
        Title = "Background",
        Callback = function(at)
            h.WindowBackground = at
            self._OuterBorder.BackgroundColor3 = at
            self._InnerFrame.BackgroundColor3 = at
        end,
    })

    local au = ar:Label("Main Color")
    local av = au:AddColorpicker("MainColor", {
        Default = h.ContentBackground,
        Title = "Main",
        Callback = function(av)
            h.ContentBackground = av
            self._ContentFrame.BackgroundColor3 = av
        end,
    })

    local aw = ar:Label("Accent Color")
    local ax = aw:AddColorpicker("AccentColor", {
        Default = h.Accent,
        Title = "Accent",
        Callback = function(ax)
            h.Accent = ax
            h.TabActive = ax
            local ay = self._InnerFrame:FindFirstChild("Header")
            if ay then
                local az = ay:FindFirstChild("Title")
                if az then
                    local aA = string.format("#%02x%02x%02x", math.floor(ax.R * 255), math.floor(ax.G * 255), math.floor(ax.B * 255))
                    local aB = string.upper(self.Title)
                    local aC = aB:find("%.[^%.]*$")
                    if aC then
                        az.Text = '<font color="#ffffff">' .. aB:sub(1, aC - 1) .. '</font><font color="' .. aA .. '">' .. aB:sub(aC) .. '</font>'
                    else
                        az.Text = '<font color="' .. aA .. '">' .. aB .. '</font>'
                    end
                end
            end
            for az, aA in ipairs(self.Tabs) do
                if aA.ActiveLine then
                    aA.ActiveLine.BackgroundColor3 = ax
                end
                if aA.Active then
                    aA.Button.TextColor3 = ax
                end
                if aA._ScrollFrame then
                    aA._ScrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = ax
                end
            end
            local function az(aA)
                for aB, aC in ipairs(aA:GetChildren()) do
                    if aC.Name == "AccentLine" and aC:IsA("Frame") then
                        aC.BackgroundColor3 = ax
                    end
                    if aC.Name == "Fill" and aC:IsA("Frame") and aC.Parent and aC.Parent.Name == "TrackBg" then
                        aC.BackgroundColor3 = ax
                    end
                    if aC.Name == "Fill" and aC:IsA("Frame") and aC.Parent and aC.Parent.Name == "InnerRing" and aC.BackgroundColor3 ~= Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18) then
                        aC.BackgroundColor3 = ax
                    end
                    if aC.Name == "ValueBox" and aC:IsA("TextBox") then
                        aC.TextColor3 = ax
                    end
                    if aC:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                        aC.ScrollBarImageColor3 = ax
                    end
                    if aC:IsA("Frame") or aC:IsA("ScreenGui") or aC:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                        az(aC)
                    end
                end
            end
            az(self._ScreenGui)
            if self._ResizeGrip then
                for aA, aB in ipairs(self._ResizeGrip:GetChildren()) do
                    if aB:IsA("Frame") and aB.Name:match("^GripLine") then
                        aB.BackgroundColor3 = ax
                    end
                end
            end
            if self._Footer then
                local aA = self._Footer:FindFirstChild("FooterLeft")
                if aA then
                    local aB = string.format("#%02x%02x%02x", math.floor(ax.R * 255), math.floor(ax.G * 255), math.floor(ax.B * 255))
                    local aC = string.upper(self.Title)
                    local aD = aC:find("%.[^%.]*$")
                    if aD then
                        aA.Text = '<font color="#ffffff">' .. aC:sub(1, aD - 1) .. '</font><font color="' .. aB .. '">' .. aC:sub(aD) .. '</font>'
                    else
                        aA.Text = '<font color="' .. aB .. '">' .. aC .. '</font>'
                    end
                end
            end
        end,
    })

    local ay = ar:Label("Outline Color")
    local az = ay:AddColorpicker("OutlineColor", {
        Default = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50),
        Title = "Outline",
        Callback = function(az)
            h.GroupBorder = az
            for aA, aB in ipairs(self._OuterBorder:GetChildren()) do
                if aB:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    aB.Color = az
                end
            end
        end,
    })

    local aA = ar:Label("Font Color")
    local aB = aA:AddColorpicker("FontColor", {
        Default = h.TextPrimary,
        Title = "Font",
        Callback = function(aB)
            h.TextPrimary = aB
            local function aC(aD)
                for aE, aF in ipairs(aD:GetChildren()) do
                    if aF:IsA("TextLabel") and aF.Name ~= "Title" and aF.Name ~= "Arrow" then
                        if aF.TextColor3 ~= h.Accent and aF.TextColor3 ~= h.TextSecondary then
                            aF.TextColor3 = aB
                        end
                    end
                    if aF:IsA("TextButton") then
                        if aF.TextColor3 ~= h.Accent and aF.TextColor3 ~= h.TextSecondary then
                            aF.TextColor3 = aB
                        end
                    end
                    aC(aF)
                end
            end
            aC(self._InnerFrame)
        end,
    })

    local aC = ar:AddInput("_settings_ThemeName", {
        Text = "Theme Name",
        Default = "",
        Placeholder = "my-theme",
        Callback = function() end,
    })

    ar:Button({
        Text = "Save Theme",
        Func = function()
            local aD = aC:GetValue()
            if aD == "" then return end
            local aE = {
                Background = {h.WindowBackground.R * 255, h.WindowBackground.G * 255, h.WindowBackground.B * 255},
                Main = {h.ContentBackground.R * 255, h.ContentBackground.G * 255, h.ContentBackground.B * 255},
                Accent = {h.Accent.R * 255, h.Accent.G * 255, h.Accent.B * 255},
                Outline = {h.GroupBorder.R * 255, h.GroupBorder.G * 255, h.GroupBorder.B * 255},
                Font = {h.TextPrimary.R * 255, h.TextPrimary.G * 255, h.TextPrimary.B * 255},
            }
            af("celsius/themes/" .. aD .. ".json", aE)
        end,
    })

    local aD = ah("celsius/themes")
    local aE = ar:AddDropdown("_settings_ThemeList", {
        Values = aD,
        Default = 1,
        Text = "Load Theme",
        Callback = function(aE)
            if aE and aE ~= "" then
                local aF = ag("celsius/themes/" .. aE .. ".json")
                if aF then
                    if aF.Background then
                        local aG = Color3.fromRGB(aF.Background[1], aF.Background[2], aF.Background[3])
                        h.WindowBackground = aG
                        self._OuterBorder.BackgroundColor3 = aG
                        self._InnerFrame.BackgroundColor3 = aG
                        at:SetValue(aG)
                    end
                    if aF.Main then
                        local aG = Color3.fromRGB(aF.Main[1], aF.Main[2], aF.Main[3])
                        h.ContentBackground = aG
                        self._ContentFrame.BackgroundColor3 = aG
                        av:SetValue(aG)
                    end
                    if aF.Accent then
                        local aG = Color3.fromRGB(aF.Accent[1], aF.Accent[2], aF.Accent[3])
                        h.Accent = aG
                        ax:SetValue(aG)
                    end
                    if aF.Outline then
                        local aG = Color3.fromRGB(aF.Outline[1], aF.Outline[2], aF.Outline[3])
                        h.GroupBorder = aG
                        az:SetValue(aG)
                    end
                    if aF.Font then
                        local aG = Color3.fromRGB(aF.Font[1], aF.Font[2], aF.Font[3])
                        h.TextPrimary = aG
                        aB:SetValue(aG)
                    end
                end
            end
        end,
    })

    task.defer(function()
        local aF = ag(ad .. "/_autoload.json")
        if aF and aF.autoload then
            local aG = ag(ad .. "/" .. aF.autoload .. ".json")
            if aG and self._registry then
                local aH = 0
                for aI, aJ in pairs(aG) do
                    local aK = self._registry[aI]
                    if aK and aK.SetValue then
                        local aL, aM = pcall(ai, aK, aJ)
                        if not aL then
                            aH = aH + 1
                        end
                    end
                end

                if self._registry["AccentColor"] then
                    pcall(function()
                        self._registry["AccentColor"]:SetValue(self._registry["AccentColor"]:GetValue())
                    end)
                end

                b.Notification("Config", aF.autoload .. " autoloaded" .. (aH > 0 and (", " .. aH .. " failed") or ""))
            else
                b.Notification("Config", "Failed to autoload " .. aF.autoload)
            end
        end
    end)

    return ak
end

function ab:AddKeybind(aj, ak)
    ak = ak or {}
    local al = ak.Default or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
    local am = ak.Text or aj
    local an = ak.Callback or function() end
    local ao = ak.ChangedCallback or nil

    local ap = al
    local aq = false

    local ar = i("Frame", {
        Name = aj .. "_Keybind",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent = self.Content,
    })

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Label",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = am,
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = ar,
    })

    local as = n(ar, "KeyOuter", UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 18), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0.5, -9))

    local at = o(as, "TextButton", {
        Name = "KeyBtn",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28),
        Text = ap == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "..." or ap.Name,
        TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = 9,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
    })

    at.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        aq = true
        at.Text = "..."
        at.TextColor3 = h.Accent
    end)

    e.InputBegan:Connect(function(au, av)
        if aq then
            if au.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
                at.Text = ap == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "..." or ap.Name
                at.TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary
                aq = false
                return
            end
            if au.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                ap = au.KeyCode
            elseif au.UserInputType.Name:match("^MouseButton%d+$") then
                ap = au.UserInputType
            else
                return
            end
            at.Text = ap.Name
            at.TextColor3 = h.TextSecondary
            aq = false
            if ao then
                ao(ap)
            end
            return
        end
        if not av and (au.KeyCode == ap or au.UserInputType == ap) and ap ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
            an(ap)
        end
    end)

    local au = {
        Instance = ar,
        SetValue = function(au, av)
            ap = av
            l(function()
                at.Text = av == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "..." or av.Name
            end)
            if ao then
                task.spawn(ao, av)
            end
        end,
        GetValue = function()
            return ap
        end,
    }

    local av = self.Tab.Window
    if av._registry then
        av._registry[aj] = au
    end

    return au
end

function b.CreateWindow(aj)
    return ac.new(aj)
end

function b.Notification(aj, ak, al)
    al = al or 3
    local am = s()
    if not am then return end

    local an = 260
    local ao = 6
    local ap = 10
    local aq = 10
    local ar = an + 20
    local as = 6
    local at = 50

    local au = am:FindFirstChild("_UILib_Notifications")
    if not au then
        au = i("ScreenGui", {
            Name = "_UILib_Notifications",
            ResetOnSpawn = false,
            ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
            Parent = am,
        })
    end

    local av = -(an + aq)

    local aw = {}
    for ax, ay in ipairs(au:GetChildren()) do
        if ay:IsA("Frame") and ay.Name == "Notification" then
            table.insert(aw, ay)
        end
    end
    while #aw >= as do
        local ax = table.remove(aw, 1)
        ax:Destroy()
    end

    local ax = ap
    for ay, az in ipairs(au:GetChildren()) do
        if az:IsA("Frame") and az.Name == "Notification" then
            az.Position = UDim2.new(1, av, 0, ax)
            local aA = az.AbsoluteSize.Y
            if aA < 10 then aA = at end
            ax = ax + aA + ao
        end
    end

    local ay = i("Frame", {
        Name = "Notification",
        Size = UDim2.new(0, an, 0, at),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, ar, 0, ax),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 14),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Parent = au,
    })
    m(ay, Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 1)

    i("Frame", {
        Name = "AccentLine",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = h.Accent,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Parent = ay,
    })

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Title",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 18),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 4),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = aj or "Notification",
        TextColor3 = h.Accent,
        FontFace = h.FontBold,
        TextSize = h.FontSize,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = ay,
    })

    i("TextLabel", {
        Name = "Desc",
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 24),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = ak or "",
        TextColor3 = h.TextPrimary,
        FontFace = h.Font,
        TextSize = h.FontSizeSmall,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextWrapped = true,
        Parent = ay,
    })

    d:Create(ay, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(1, av, 0, ax),
    }):Play()

    task.delay(al, function()
        local az = d:Create(ay, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Position = UDim2.new(1, ar, 0, ay.Position.Y.Offset),
        })
        az:Play()
        az.Completed:Wait()
        ay:Destroy()

        local aA = ap
        for aB, aC in ipairs(au:GetChildren()) do
            if aC:IsA("Frame") and aC.Name == "Notification" then
                d:Create(aC, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    Position = UDim2.new(1, av, 0, aA),
                }):Play()
                local aD = aC.AbsoluteSize.Y
                if aD < 10 then aD = at end
                aA = aA + aD + ao
            end
        end
    end)
end

return b end function a.b()
LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))

local aa = game:GetService("RunService")
local ab = {}

ab.BONES = {
    { "head", "torso" },
    { "torso", "shoulder1" },
    { "shoulder1", "arm1" },
    { "torso", "shoulder2" },
    { "shoulder2", "arm2" },
    { "torso", "hip1_virtual" },
    { "hip1_virtual", "hip1" },
    { "hip1", "leg1" },
    { "torso", "hip2_virtual" },
    { "hip2_virtual", "hip2" },
    { "hip2", "leg2" },
}

ab.WORLD_TYPES = {
    { name = "Barricade", names = { "DoorBarricade" }, class = "BarricadeItem" },
    { name = "Reinforcement", names = { "Reinforcement" }, class = "ReinforceItem" },

    { name = "Frag Grenade", names = { "FragGrenade" }, class = "FragGrenade" },
