ab._world_type_lookup = {}

for ad, ae in ipairs(ab.WORLD_TYPES) do
    ab.Config.World.TypeList[#ab.Config.World.TypeList + 1] = ae.name
    ab.Config.World.Types[ae.name] = false
    for af, ag in ipairs(ae.names) do
        ab._world_type_lookup[ag] = ae
    end
end

ab._objects = {}
ab._world_objects = {}
ab._world_icons = {}
ab._chams_originals = {}
ab._chams_highlights = {}
ab._chams_cache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
ab._bone_cache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

ab._get_gui = function()
    if ab._gui_container then return ab._gui_container end
    local ad, ae = pcall(gethui)
    if ad and typeof(ae) == "Instance" and ae:IsA("ScreenGui") then
        pcall(function() ae.IgnoreGuiInset = true end)
        ab._gui_container = ae
        return ae
    end
    local af = game:GetService("Players")
    local ag = ad and typeof(ae) == "Instance" and ae or game:GetService("CoreGui") or (af.LocalPlayer and af.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui)
    local ah = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    ah.Name = "EspLabels"
    ah.ResetOnSpawn = false
    ah.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    if ag and not pcall(function() ah.Parent = ag end) then
        ah.Parent = af.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui
    end
    ab._gui_container = ah
    return ah
end

ab._create_world_object = function(ad)
    local ae = ab._get_gui()
    local af = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    af.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 20)
    af.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    af.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    af.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    af.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    af.TextSize = 13
    af.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json")
    af.Visible = false
    af.Parent = ae

    local ag = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    ag.Size = UDim2.new(0, ab.Config.World.Icons.Size, 0, ab.Config.World.Icons.Size)
    ag.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    ag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ag.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    ag.Visible = false
    ag.Parent = ae

    local ah = { name_text = af, icon_image = ag }
    ab._world_objects[ad] = ah
    return ah
end

ab._remove_world_object = function(ad)
    local ae = ab._world_objects[ad]
    if not ae then return end
    ae.name_text:Destroy()
    ae.icon_image:Destroy()
    ab._world_objects[ad] = nil
end

ab._hide_world_object = function(ad)
    ad.name_text.Visible = false
    ad.icon_image.Visible = false
end

ab._create_text_label = function(ad)
    local ae = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    ae.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ae.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    ae.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    ae.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    ae.TextSize = 13
    ae.TextWrapped = false
    ae.FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json")
    ae.Visible = false
    ae.Parent = ad
    ae.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
    ae.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    ae.ZIndex = 3
    return ae
end

ab._new_frame = function(ad)
    local ae = Instance.new("Frame")
    ae.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    ae.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ae.Visible = false
    ae.Parent = ad
    return ae
end

ab._new_line = function(ad)
    local ae = ab._new_frame(ad)
    ae.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    return ae
end

ab._set_line = function(ad, ae, af, ag, ah)
    local ai = af - ae
    local aj = ai.Magnitude
    ad.Position = UDim2.new(0, (ae.X + af.X) * 0.5, 0, (ae.Y + af.Y) * 0.5)
    ad.Size = UDim2.new(0, aj, 0, ag)
    ad.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(ai.Y, ai.X))
    ad.BackgroundColor3 = ah
    ad.Visible = true
end

ab._resolve_bone = function(ad, ae)
    local af = ad.viewmodels
    local ag = af and ab._bone_cache[af]
    if ag then
        local ah = ag[ae]
        if ah ~= nil then
            return ah or nil
        end
    end

    local ah = string.lower(ae)
    local ai = ad.body_parts
    local aj = ai and ai[ae]

    if aj == nil and ai then
        for ak, al in ai do
            if type(ak) == "string" and typeof(al) == "Instance" and string.lower(ak) == ah then
                aj = al
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if aj == nil and ai and ah == "torso" then
        aj = ai.UpperTorso or ai.LowerTorso
    end

    if typeof(aj) ~= "Instance" then
        aj = nil
        if af then
            aj = af:FindFirstChild(ae)
            if not aj then
                for ak, al in af:GetChildren() do
                    if al:IsA("BasePart") and string.lower(al.Name) == ah then
                        aj = al
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if af then
        if not ag then
            ag = {}
            ab._bone_cache[af] = ag
        end
        ag[ae] = aj or false
    end

    return aj
end

ab._create_object = function(ad)
    local ae = ab._get_gui()

    local af = ab._create_text_label(ae)
    af.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    af.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center

    local ag = ab._create_text_label(ae)
    ag.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    ag.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center

    local ah = ab._create_text_label(ae)
    ah.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    ah.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

    local ai = {
        name_text = af,
        weapon_text = ag,
        health_text = ah,
        health_bar_outline = ab._new_frame(ae),
        health_bar_fill = ab._new_frame(ae),
        box_fill = ab._new_frame(ae),
        corner_lines = {},
        bones = {},
    }

    ai.health_bar_outline.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    ai.health_bar_outline.ZIndex = 1

    ai.health_bar_fill.ZIndex = 2

    ai.box_fill.ZIndex = 1

    for aj = 1, 8 do
        ai.corner_lines[#ai.corner_lines + 1] = ab._new_line(ae)
    end

    for aj = 1, #ab.BONES do
        ai.bones[#ai.bones + 1] = ab._new_line(ae)
    end

    ab._objects[ad] = ai
    return ai
end

ab._remove_object = function(ad)
    local ae = ab._objects[ad]
    if not ae then return end
    for af, ag in ae do
        if af == "bones" or af == "corner_lines" then
            for ah, ai in ag do ai:Destroy() end
        elseif typeof(ag) == "Instance" then
            ag:Destroy()
        end
    end
    ab._objects[ad] = nil
end

ab._hide_object = function(ad)
    if not ad then return end
    for ae, af in ad do
        if ae == "bones" or ae == "corner_lines" then
            for ag, ah in af do ah.Visible = false end
        else
            af.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

ab._apply_chams = function(ad, ae, af, ag, ah)
    if not ah then return end
    local ai = ag == nil and 1 or ag
    local aj = Color3.new(ae.R * ai, ae.G * ai, ae.B * ai)

    local ak = ab._chams_cache[ad]
    if ak and ak.model == ah then
        if ak.color == aj and ak.walls == af then
            return
        end
    else
        if ak then
            ab._remove_chams(ad)
        end
        ak = { model = ah, parts = {} }
        for al, am in ah:GetDescendants() do
            ak.parts[#ak.parts + 1] = am
        end
        ab._chams_cache[ad] = ak
    end

    ak.color = aj
    ak.walls = af

    local al = ab._chams_originals[ad]
    if not al then
        al = {}
        ab._chams_originals[ad] = al
    end

    for am, an in ak.parts do
        local ao = al[an]
        if an:IsA("BasePart") then
            if not ao then al[an] = { material = an.Material, color = an.Color } end
            an.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            an.Color = aj
        elseif an:IsA("Shirt") then
            if not ao then al[an] = { shirt_template = an.ShirtTemplate } end
            an.ShirtTemplate = ""
        elseif an:IsA("Pants") then
            if not ao then al[an] = { pants_template = an.PantsTemplate } end
            an.PantsTemplate = ""
        elseif an:IsA("SpecialMesh") or an:IsA("FileMesh") then
            if not ao then al[an] = { texture_id = an.TextureId, vertex_color = an.VertexColor } end
            an.TextureId = "rbxassetid://11111"
            an.VertexColor = Vector3.new(aj.R, aj.G, aj.B)
        elseif an:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            if not ao then al[an] = { color_map = an.ColorMap } end
            an.ColorMap = ""
        elseif an:IsA("Decal") or an:IsA("Texture") then
            if not ao then al[an] = { transparency = an.Transparency } end
            an.Transparency = 1
        end
    end

    local am = ab._chams_highlights[ad]
    if af then
        if not am or am.Adornee ~= ah or not am.Parent then
            if am then
                am:Destroy()
            end
            am = Instance.new("Highlight")
            am.Name = "ChamHighlight"
            ab._chams_highlights[ad] = am
            am.Adornee = ah
            am.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            am.OutlineTransparency = 1
            am.FillTransparency = 0.5
            am.Parent = ah
        end
        am.FillColor = aj
    elseif am then
        am:Destroy()
        ab._chams_highlights[ad] = nil
    end
end

ab._remove_chams = function(ad)
    ab._chams_cache[ad] = nil
    local ae = ab._chams_originals[ad]
    if ae then
        for af, ag in ae do
            if af:IsA("BasePart") then
                af.Material = ag.material
                af.Color = ag.color
            elseif af:IsA("Shirt") and ag.shirt_template then
                af.ShirtTemplate = ag.shirt_template
            elseif af:IsA("Pants") and ag.pants_template then
                af.PantsTemplate = ag.pants_template
            elseif af:IsA("SpecialMesh") or af:IsA("FileMesh") then
                af.TextureId = ag.texture_id
                af.VertexColor = ag.vertex_color
            elseif af:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
                af.ColorMap = ag.color_map
            elseif af:IsA("Decal") or af:IsA("Texture") then
                af.Transparency = ag.transparency
            end
        end
        ab._chams_originals[ad] = nil
    end
    local af = ab._chams_highlights[ad]
    if af then
        af:Destroy()
        ab._chams_highlights[ad] = nil
    end
end

ab._update_char = function(ad, ae, af, ag)
    local ah = game:GetService("Workspace").CurrentCamera
    if not ah then
        ab._hide_object(ab._objects[ad.character])
        return
    end

    if not ad.torso or not ad.head or not ad.root_part or ad.health <= 0 then
        ab._hide_object(ab._objects[ad.character])
        ab._remove_chams(ad.character)
        return
    end

    local ai = (ah.CFrame.Position - ad.torso.Position).Magnitude

    if ae.MaxDistance.Enable and ai > ae.MaxDistance.Range then
        ab._hide_object(ab._objects[ad.character])
        return
    end

    if ae.TeamCheck and ae.TeamCheck.Enable and not ad.enemy then
        ab._hide_object(ab._objects[ad.character])
        return
    end

    if ae.VisibleCheck and ae.VisibleCheck.Enable and af and not af(ad) then
        ab._hide_object(ab._objects[ad.character])
        return
    end

    if ae.Chams and ae.Chams.Enable then
        local aj = ae.Chams
        local ak = ad.viewmodels or (ag and ag(ad.character))
        pcall(ab._apply_chams, ad.character, aj.Color, aj.ThroughWalls, aj.Brightness, ak)
    else
        pcall(ab._remove_chams, ad.character)
    end

    local aj, ak = ah:WorldToViewportPoint(ad.head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0))
    local al = ah:WorldToViewportPoint(ad.torso.Position)

    if not ak then
        ab._hide_object(ab._objects[ad.character])
        return
    end

    local am = ab._objects[ad.character] or ab._create_object(ad.character)

    local an, ao, ap, aq

    if ad.body_parts then
        local ar, as = math.huge, math.huge
        local at, au = -math.huge, -math.huge

        for av, aw in ad.body_parts do
            local ax, ay = ah:WorldToViewportPoint(aw.Position)
            if ay then
                if ax.X < ar then ar = ax.X end
                if ax.X > at then at = ax.X end
                if ax.Y < as then as = ax.Y end
                if ax.Y > au then au = ax.Y end
            end
        end

        if ar ~= math.huge then
            local av = math.max(3, (au - as) * 0.15)
            aq = as - av
            an = au - as + av * 2
            ao = at - ar + av * 2
            ap = ar - av
            if ao < an * 0.35 then
                ao = an * 0.5
                ap = al.X - ao / 2
            end
        else
            local av = ah:WorldToViewportPoint(ad.torso.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
            an = av.Y - aj.Y
            ao = an * 0.6
            ap = al.X - ao / 2
            aq = aj.Y
        end
    else
        local ar = ah:WorldToViewportPoint(ad.torso.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
        an = ar.Y - aj.Y
        ao = an * 0.6
        ap = al.X - ao / 2
        aq = aj.Y
    end

    if ae.Name.Enable or ae.Distance.Enable then
        local ar = ""
        if ae.Name.Enable then
            ar = ad.player.Name
        end
        if ae.Distance.Enable then
            ar = ar .. " [" .. math.floor(ai) .. "m]"
        end
        am.name_text.Text = ar
        am.name_text.Position = UDim2.new(0, ap + ao / 2, 0, aq - 2)
        am.name_text.TextColor3 = ae.Name.Color
        am.name_text.Visible = true
    else
        am.name_text.Visible = false
    end

    if ae.Weapon and ae.Weapon.Enable and ad.weapon_name ~= "" then
        am.weapon_text.Text = ad.weapon_name
        am.weapon_text.Position = UDim2.new(0, al.X, 0, aq + an + 2)
        am.weapon_text.TextColor3 = ae.Weapon.Color
        am.weapon_text.Visible = true
    else
        am.weapon_text.Visible = false
    end

    if ae.HealthBar.Enable then
        local ar = math.clamp(ad.health / ad.max_health, 0, 1)
        local as = ap - 4
        local at = an
        local au = at * ar

        am.health_bar_outline.Position = UDim2.new(0, as - 1.5, 0, aq)
        am.health_bar_outline.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, at)
        am.health_bar_outline.Visible = true

        am.health_bar_fill.Position = UDim2.new(0, as - 0.5, 0, aq + at - au)
        am.health_bar_fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, au)
        if ae.HealthBar.Gradient then
            am.health_bar_fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255 * (1 - ar), 255 * ar, 0)
        else
            am.health_bar_fill.BackgroundColor3 = ae.HealthBar.Color
        end
        am.health_bar_fill.Visible = true

        if ae.HealthText.Enable then
            am.health_text.Text = tostring(math.floor(ad.health))
            am.health_text.Position = UDim2.new(0, as - 3, 0, aq + at - au - 6)
            am.health_text.TextColor3 = ae.HealthText.Color
            am.health_text.Visible = true
        else
            am.health_text.Visible = false
        end
    else
        am.health_bar_outline.Visible = false
        am.health_bar_fill.Visible = false
        am.health_text.Visible = false
    end

    if ae.Box and ae.Box.Enable then
        if ae.Box.Mode == "Corner" then
            local ar = ao * 0.25
            local as = an * 0.25

            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[1], Vector2.new(ap, aq), Vector2.new(ap + ar, aq), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[2], Vector2.new(ap, aq), Vector2.new(ap, aq + as), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[3], Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq), Vector2.new(ap + ao - ar, aq), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[4], Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq), Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq + as), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[5], Vector2.new(ap, aq + an), Vector2.new(ap + ar, aq + an), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[6], Vector2.new(ap, aq + an), Vector2.new(ap, aq + an - as), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[7], Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq + an), Vector2.new(ap + ao - ar, aq + an), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[8], Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq + an), Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq + an - as), 1, ae.Box.Color)
        else
            for ar = 5, 8 do
                am.corner_lines[ar].Visible = false
            end
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[1], Vector2.new(ap, aq), Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[2], Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq), Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq + an), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[3], Vector2.new(ap + ao, aq + an), Vector2.new(ap, aq + an), 1, ae.Box.Color)
            ab._set_line(am.corner_lines[4], Vector2.new(ap, aq + an), Vector2.new(ap, aq), 1, ae.Box.Color)
        end

        if ae.Box.Fill then
            am.box_fill.Position = UDim2.new(0, ap, 0, aq)
            am.box_fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, ao, 0, an)
            am.box_fill.BackgroundColor3 = ae.Box.FillColor
            am.box_fill.BackgroundTransparency = ae.Box.FillTransparency
            am.box_fill.Visible = true
        else
            am.box_fill.Visible = false
        end
    else
        am.box_fill.Visible = false
        for ar = 1, 8 do
            am.corner_lines[ar].Visible = false
        end
    end

    if ae.Skeleton and ae.Skeleton.Enable and (ad.viewmodels or ad.body_parts) then
        local ar = ab._resolve_bone(ad, "torso")
        local as = ab._resolve_bone(ad, "head")
        if ar and ar:IsA("BasePart") then
            local at = ar.CFrame.UpVector
            local au = ar.Position - at * 0.8
            local av = ar.Position + at * 0.7
            local aw = as and as:IsA("BasePart") and as.CFrame.RightVector or ar.CFrame.RightVector
            local ax = au + aw * 0.35 - at * 0.15
            local ay = au - aw * 0.35 - at * 0.15

            for az, aA in ab.BONES do
                local aB, aC = aA[1], aA[2]
                local aD, aE
                local aF = true

                if aB == "torso" and aC == "hip1_virtual" then
                    aD, aE = au, ax
                elseif aB == "torso" and aC == "hip2_virtual" then
                    aD, aE = au, ay
                elseif aB == "hip1_virtual" and aC == "hip1" then
                    aD = ax
                    local aG = ab._resolve_bone(ad, "hip1")
                    if aG and aG:IsA("BasePart") then
                        aE = aG.Position
                    else
                        aF = false
                    end
                elseif aB == "hip2_virtual" and aC == "hip2" then
                    aD = ay
                    local aG = ab._resolve_bone(ad, "hip2")
                    if aG and aG:IsA("BasePart") then
                        aE = aG.Position
                    else
                        aF = false
                    end
                else
                    local aG = ab._resolve_bone(ad, aB)
                    local aH = ab._resolve_bone(ad, aC)
                    if aG and aG:IsA("BasePart") and aH and aH:IsA("BasePart") then
                        aD, aE = aG.Position, aH.Position
                        if aG.Name == "torso" and (aH.Name == "shoulder1" or aH.Name == "shoulder2") then
                            if as and as:IsA("BasePart") then
                                aD = as.Position + (au - as.Position) * 0.35
                            else
                                aD = av
                            end
                        end
                        if aG.Name == "head" and aH.Name == "torso" then
                            aE = au
                        end
                    else
                        aF = false
                    end
                end

                if aF then
                    local aG = ah:WorldToViewportPoint(aD)
                    local aH = ah:WorldToViewportPoint(aE)
                    if aG.Z > 0 and aH.Z > 0 then
                        ab._set_line(am.bones[az], Vector2.new(aG.X, aG.Y), Vector2.new(aH.X, aH.Y), ae.Skeleton.Thickness, ae.Skeleton.Color)
                    else
                        am.bones[az].Visible = false
                    end
                else
                    am.bones[az].Visible = false
                end
            end
        else
            for at = 1, #am.bones do
                am.bones[at].Visible = false
            end
        end
    else
        for ar = 1, #am.bones do
            am.bones[ar].Visible = false
        end
    end
end

ab.load = function(ad, ae, af)
    if ab._connection then
        ab._connection:Disconnect()
    end
    local ag = function()
        local ag = game:GetService("Workspace").CurrentCamera
        if not ag then
            for ah, ai in ab._objects do
                ab._hide_object(ai)
            end
            for ah, ai in ab._world_objects do
                ab._hide_world_object(ai)
            end
            return
        end
        local ah = {}

        if ab.Config.Player.Enable then
            for ai, aj in ad() do
                ah[aj.character] = true
                ab._update_char(aj, ab.Config.Player, ae, af)
            end
        end

        local ai = {}
        for aj, ak in ab._objects do
            if not ah[aj] then
                ab._hide_object(ak)
                ai[aj] = true
            end
        end
        for aj in ai do
            ab._remove_chams(aj)
            ab._remove_object(aj)
        end

        local aj = {}

        if ab.Config.World.Enable then
            local ak = game:GetService("Workspace")
            local al = ag.CFrame.Position

            if ab.Config.World.Weapons.Enable then
                local am = ak:FindFirstChild("Ignore")
                local an = am and am:FindFirstChild("GunDrop")
                if an then
                    for ao, ap in an:GetChildren() do
                        local aq = ap:IsA("Model") and ap:GetPivot().Position or ap.Position
                        local ar = ap.Name:gsub("%s*Main$", "")
                        aj["weapon_" .. ar] = { pos = aq, name = ar, color = ab.Config.World.Weapons.Color }
                    end
                end
            end

            if ab.Config.World.Grenades.Enable then
                local am = ak:FindFirstChild("Ignore")
                local an = am and am:FindFirstChild("Misc")
                for ao, ap in ipairs({ ak, an }) do
                    for aq, ar in ap:GetChildren() do
                        if ar.Name:find("Projectile_") then
                            local as = ar:IsA("Model") and ar:GetPivot().Position or ar.Position
                            aj["grenade_" .. ar.Name] = { pos = as, name = ar.Name:gsub("Projectile_", ""):gsub("_", " "), color = ab.Config.World.Grenades.Color }
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            for am, an in ak:GetChildren() do
                local ao = ab._world_type_lookup[an.Name]
                if ao and ab.Config.World.Types[ao.name] then
                    local ap
                    if an:IsA("Model") then
                        ap = an:GetPivot().Position
                    elseif an:IsA("BasePart") then
                        ap = an.Position
                    end
                    if ap then
                        aj[an] = { pos = ap, name = ao.name, color = ab.Config.World.Name.Color, class = ao.class }
                    end
                end
            end

            for am, an in aj do
                local ao, ap = ag:WorldToViewportPoint(an.pos)
                if ap then
                    local aq = (al - an.pos).Magnitude
                    local ar = ab._world_objects[am] or ab._create_world_object(am)
                    local as = ab.Config.World.Name.Enable and an.name or ""
                    if ab.Config.World.Distance.Enable then
                        as = as .. (as ~= "" and " " or "") .. "[" .. math.floor(aq) .. "m]"
                    end
                    ar.icon_image.Visible = false
                    if an.class and ab.Config.World.Icons.Enable then
                        local at = ab._world_icons[an.class]
                        if at == nil then
                            local au, av = pcall(function()
                                return grab("Items").get_item_class(an.class)
                            end)
                            at = au and type(av) == "table" and av.icon or false
                            ab._world_icons[an.class] = at
                        end
                        if at then
                            ar.icon_image.Image = at
                            ar.icon_image.Size = UDim2.new(0, ab.Config.World.Icons.Size, 0, ab.Config.World.Icons.Size)
                            ar.icon_image.Position = UDim2.new(0, ao.X, 0, ao.Y)
                            ar.icon_image.Visible = true
                        end
                    end
                    ar.name_text.Text = as
                    ar.name_text.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    ar.name_text.TextColor3 = an.color
                    ar.name_text.Visible = as ~= ""
                    if ar.icon_image.Visible then
                        ar.name_text.Position = UDim2.new(0, ao.X, 0, ao.Y + ar.icon_image.Size.Y.Offset / 2 + 2)
                    else
                        ar.name_text.Position = UDim2.new(0, ao.X, 0, ao.Y)
                    end
                else
                    if ab._world_objects[am] then
                        ab._hide_world_object(ab._world_objects[am])
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        ai = {}
        for ak, al in ab._world_objects do
            if not aj[ak] then
                ab._hide_world_object(al)
                ai[ak] = true
            end
        end
        for ak in ai do
            ab._remove_world_object(ak)
        end
    end

    if aa.PreRender then
        ab._connection = aa.PreRender:Connect(ag)
    else
        aa:BindToRenderStep("celsius_esp", Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value + 1, ag)
        ab._connection = { Disconnect = function() aa:UnbindFromRenderStep("celsius_esp") end }
    end
end

ab.unload = function()
    if ab._connection then
        ab._connection:Disconnect()
        ab._connection = nil
    end
    for ad in ab._objects do
        ab._remove_object(ad)
    end
    for ad in ab._chams_originals do
        ab._remove_chams(ad)
    end
    for ad in ab._world_objects do
        ab._remove_world_object(ad)
    end
    if ab._gui_container then
        ab._gui_container:Destroy()
        ab._gui_container = nil
    end
end

return ab end function a.c()
LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))

local aa = {}
aa.__index = aa

aa.new = function()
	local ab = setmetatable({}, aa)

	ab.registry = {}
	ab.game_mt = {
		__namecall = function(ac, ...) end,
		__index = function(ac, ad: string) end,
		__newindex = function(ac, ad: string, ae: any) end,
	}

	if getrawmetatable then
		ab.game_mt = getrawmetatable(game)
	end

	return ab
end

aa.register_hook = function(
	ab,
	ac: string,
	ad: (...any) -> ...any,
	ae: (original: (...any) -> ...any,...any) -> ...any
): { original: (...any) -> ...any, reference: (...any) -> ...any }
	local af: (...any) -> ...any

	if ab.registry[ac] then
		hookfunction(ab.registry[ac].reference, ab.registry[ac].original)
	end

	af = hookfunction(ad, function(...)
		return ae(af, ...)
	end)

	ab.registry[ac] = {
		original = af or ad,
		reference = ad,
	}

	return ab.registry[ac]
end

aa.unregister_hook = function(ab, ac: string)
	if ab.registry[ac] then
		hookfunction(ab.registry[ac].reference, ab.registry[ac].original)
	end

	ab.registry[ac] = nil
end

aa.clear_hooks = function(ab)
	local ac = 0
	for ad in next, ab.registry do
		ac += 1
	end

	for ad, ae in next, ab.registry do
		hookfunction(ae.reference, ae.original)
	end

	ab.registry = {}
end

return aa
end function a.d()
local aa = {}

aa.load = function()
    aa._old_send = grab("NetworkClient").send
    grab("NetworkClient").send = function(ab, ac, ...)
        if ac == "flaguser" or ac == "debug" then
            return
        end
        return aa._old_send(ab, ac, ...)
    end
end

aa.unload = function()
    if aa._old_send then
        grab("NetworkClient").send = aa._old_send
        aa._old_send = nil
    end
end

return aa
end function a.e()
local aa = game:GetService("Players")
local ab = game:GetService("Workspace")

local ac = {}

ac.get_players = function()
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local ad = {}
    local ae = grab("ReplicationInterface")

    for af, ag in aa:GetPlayers() do
        if ag == aa.LocalPlayer then continue end

        local ah = ae.getEntry(ag)
        if not ah or not ah:isAlive() or not ah:isReady() then continue end

        local ai = ah:getThirdPersonObject()
        if not ai or not ai._head then continue end

        local aj = ai:getCharacterHash() or {}
        local ak = ah:getPosition()

        ad[#ad + 1] = {
            player = ag,
            character = ag,
            viewmodels = ai:getCharacterModel(),
            torso = aj.Torso or { Position = ak },
            head = aj.Head or { Position = ak },
            root_part = ai._rootPart or { Position = ak },
            health = ah:getHealth(),
            max_health = select(2, ah:getHealth()),
            weapon_name = ah:getWeaponObject() and ah:getWeaponObject().weaponName or "",
            enemy = ah:isEnemy(),
            team = "",
            body_parts = aj,
        }
    end
    return ad
end

ac.is_player_visible = function(ad)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local ae = ab:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local af = ab.CurrentCamera
    if not ae or not af or not ad.head then
        return true
    end

    local ag = RaycastParams.new()
    ag.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
    ag.FilterDescendantsInstances = { ae }

    local ah = af.CFrame.Position
    local ai = ad.head.Position - ah
    local aj = ab:Raycast(ah, ai, ag)

    return not (aj and aj.Distance < ai.Magnitude - 5)
end

ac.get_character_model = function(ad)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local ae = grab("ReplicationInterface").getEntry(ad)
    local af = ae and ae:getThirdPersonObject()
    return af and af:getCharacterModel() or nil
end

return ac
end function a.f()
local aa = {}
aa._triggerbot_last_fire = 0
aa._triggerbot_firing = false
aa._autoshooting = false
aa._fov_circle = Drawing.new("Circle")
aa._fov_circle.Filled = false
aa._fov_circle.Visible = false
aa._fov_circle.NumSides = 64
aa._target_line = Drawing.new("Line")
aa._target_line.Visible = false

aa.setup = function(ab, ac, ad)
    local ae = grab("BulletInterface").newBullet
    local af = grab("ReplicationInterface")
    local ag = grab("GameClock")
    local ah = grab("Animation")
    local ai = af.playerHitCheck
    local aj = grab("OcclusionCheck")
    local ak = aj and aj.new(nil, 0.9)

    if not ak then
        warn("combat: OcclusionCheck unavailable, visible checks disabled")
    end

    ad:register_hook("silent_aim", ae, function(al, am)
        setstackhidden(al, true)
        if ab.combat.silent_aim.enable and aa._target then
            local an = am.velocity.Magnitude
            am.velocity = an * (aa._target - am.position).Unit
        end
        return al(am)
    end)

    ad:register_hook("silent_aim_net", grab("NetworkClient").send, function(al, am, an, ...)
        setstackhidden(al, true)
        if an == "newbullets" and ab.combat.silent_aim.enable and aa._target then
            local ao, ap, aq = ...
            for ar, as in ap.bullets do
                as[1] = (aa._target - ap.firepos).Unit
            end
            return al(am, an, ao, ap, aq)
        end
        return al(am, an, ...)
    end)

    aa._connection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local al = grab("CharacterInterface").getCharacterObject()
        if not al or not al._weaponController then return end
        local am = al._weaponController:getActiveWeapon()
        if not am or am:getWeaponType() ~= "Firearm" then return end

        local an = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local ao = Vector2.new(an.ViewportSize.X / 2, an.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
        local ap = grab("CameraInterface").getActiveCamera("MainCamera")

        aa._target = nil

        if ab.combat.silent_aim.enable then
            local aq = an.CFrame.Position
            local ar = math.huge
            local as

            for at, au in ac.get_players() do
                if ab.combat.silent_aim.team_check and not au.enemy then continue end

                local av = au.body_parts[ab.combat.silent_aim.hitpart]
                local aw = av and av.Position or au.head.Position
                local ax, ay = an:WorldToViewportPoint(aw)
                if not ay then continue end

                local az = (Vector2.new(ax.X, ax.Y) - ao).Magnitude
                if az > ab.combat.silent_aim.fov.size then continue end

                if ab.combat.silent_aim.max_distance.enable and (aw - aq).Magnitude > ab.combat.silent_aim.max_distance.range then continue end

                if ab.combat.silent_aim.visible_check and ak and not ak:checkOcclusion(aq, aw - aq) then continue end

                if az < ar then
                    ar = az
                    aa._target = aw
                    as = au
                end
            end

            if as then
                local at
                if ab.combat.silent_aim.backtrack.enable then
                    local au, av = pcall(function()
                        local au = af.getEntry(as.player)
                        local av = au._smoothReplication:getFrame(ag.getTime() - ab.combat.silent_aim.backtrack.time / 1000)
                        local aw = as.body_parts[ab.combat.silent_aim.hitpart] or as.head
                        local ax = as.root_part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(aw.Position)
                        return (CFrame.new(av.position) * CFrame.Angles(av.angles.X, av.angles.Y, 0)):PointToWorldSpace(ax)
                    end)
                    at = au and av or nil
                else
                    local au, av = pcall(ai, af, aq, aa._target)
                    if au then
                        local aw
                        for ax, ay in av do
                            local az = ay.bestDirectNearestPosition or ay.bestNearestPosition
                            local aA = az and (az - aq).Magnitude
                            if aA and (not aw or aA < aw) then
                                at = az
                                aw = aA
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if at then
                    aa._target = at
                end
            end
        end

        if ab.combat.rage.autoshoot then
            if aa._target and not aa._autoshooting then
                am:shoot(true)
                aa._autoshooting = true
            elseif not aa._target and aa._autoshooting then
                am:shoot(false)
                aa._autoshooting = false
            end
        elseif aa._autoshooting then
            aa._autoshooting = false
        end

        if ab.combat.triggerbot.enable then
            local aq = false

            for ar, as in ac.get_players() do
                if ab.combat.triggerbot.team_check and not as.enemy then continue end

                local at = as.head.Position
                local au, av = an:WorldToViewportPoint(at)
                if not av then continue end

                if ab.combat.triggerbot.visible_check and ak and not ak:checkOcclusion(an.CFrame.Position, at - an.CFrame.Position) then continue end

                if (Vector2.new(au.X, au.Y) - ao).Magnitude > 50 then continue end

                aq = true
                break
            end

            if aq then
                local ar = os.clock()
                if ar - aa._triggerbot_last_fire >= ab.combat.triggerbot.delay / 1000 then
                    aa._triggerbot_last_fire = ar
                    if not aa._triggerbot_firing then
                        aa._triggerbot_firing = true
                        mouse1press()
                    end
                end
            elseif aa._triggerbot_firing then
                aa._triggerbot_firing = false
                mouse1release()
            end
        end

        aa._fov_circle.Position = ao
        aa._fov_circle.Radius = ab.combat.silent_aim.fov.size
        aa._fov_circle.Color = ab.combat.silent_aim.fov.color
        aa._fov_circle.Thickness = ab.combat.silent_aim.fov.thickness
        aa._fov_circle.Visible = ab.combat.silent_aim.enable and ab.combat.silent_aim.fov.enable

        if aa._target and ab.combat.silent_aim.target_line.enable then
            local aq = an:WorldToViewportPoint(aa._target)
            aa._target_line.From = ao
            aa._target_line.To = Vector2.new(aq.X, aq.Y)
            aa._target_line.Color = ab.combat.silent_aim.target_line.color
            aa._target_line.Thickness = ab.combat.silent_aim.target_line.width
            aa._target_line.Visible = true
        else
            aa._target_line.Visible = false
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.no_sway then
            al.sway.posSpring.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            al.sway.posSpring.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            al.sway.angSpring.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            al.sway.angSpring.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.no_equip_time then
            al:getSpring("equipspring").p = 0
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.no_suppression then
            if ap and ap._blurObject then
                ap._blurObject._blurSpring.p = 0
                ap._blurObject._blurSpring.v = 0
                ap._blurObject._blur.Size = 0
            end
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.no_camera_bob then
            al._speed = 0
            al._distance = 0
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.no_recoil then
            for aq, ar in am._translationSprings._vector3Springs do ar.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) ar.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
            for aq, ar in am._rotationSprings._vector3Springs do ar.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) ar.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
            for aq, ar in ap._cameraBodySprings._vector3Springs do ar.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) ar.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
            for aq, ar in ap._cameraHeadSprings._vector3Springs do ar.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) ar.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
            am._spreadSpring.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            am._spreadSpring.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            am._chokeSpring.p = 0
            am._chokeSpring.v = 0
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.no_spread then
            am._spreadSpring.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            am._spreadSpring.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            am._chokeSpring.p = 0
            am._chokeSpring.v = 0
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.rapid_fire then
            am._nextShot = 0
            am._needRechambering = false
            am._heatFirerateSpring.p = 1
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.no_flinch then
            ap._damageSpring.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            ap._damageSpring.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            ap._damageSpring.a = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            ap._suppressionSpring.p = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            ap._suppressionSpring.v = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            ap._suppressionSpring.a = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.instant_ads then
            local aq = al:getSpring("masteraimspring")
            aq.p = aq.t
            aq.v = 0
            local ar = al:getSpring("zoommodspring")
            ar.p = ar.t
            ar.v = 0
            am._aimArmSpring.p = am._aimArmSpring.t
            am._aimArmSpring.v = 0
            for as, at in am._activeAimStats do
                at.sightaimspring.p = at.sightaimspring.t
                at.sightaimspring.v = 0
                at.sightmagspring.p = at.sightmagspring.t
                at.sightmagspring.v = 0
            end
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.instant_reload and am:isStateReloading() then
            am:fireInput("reloadFinish", ag.getTime())
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.no_rechamber then
            am._needRechambering = false
            am._bolting = false
            if am._magCount > 0 and am._chamberState:isState("unchambered") then
                am:fireInput("chamberStart", ag.getTime())
            end
            if am._chamberState:isState("chambering") then
                am:fireInput("chamberFinish", ag.getTime())
            end
        end

        if ab.combat.gun_mods.reload_cancel and al.reloading and not am:isStateReloading() then
            al.thread:clear()
            al.animating = false
            al.reloading = false
            am._reloadSpring.t = 0
            al.thread:add(ah.reset(am._animData, am._reloadCancelTime, am:getWeaponStat("keepanimvisibility")))
        end

        if ab.combat.aim_assist.enable then
            local aq = ab.combat.aim_assist.strength
            ap._touchAssistInputStrength = aq
            ap._touchAssistFollowStrength = aq
            ap._controllerAssistInputStrength = aq
            ap._controllerAssistFollowStrength = aq
        else
            ap._touchAssistInputStrength = 0
            ap._touchAssistFollowStrength = 0
            ap._controllerAssistInputStrength = 0
            ap._controllerAssistFollowStrength = 0
        end
    end)
end

aa.unload = function(ab)
    if aa._connection then
        aa._connection:Disconnect()
        aa._connection = nil
    end
    aa._fov_circle:Remove()
    aa._target_line:Remove()
    if aa._autoshooting then
        local ac = grab("CharacterInterface").getCharacterObject()
        local ad = ac and ac._weaponController and ac._weaponController:getActiveWeapon()
        if ad then
            ad:shoot(false)
        end
        aa._autoshooting = false
    end
    ab:unregister_hook("silent_aim")
    ab:unregister_hook("silent_aim_net")
end

return aa end function a.g()
local aa = a.load('c')
local ab = {}
ab._features = nil
ab._connection = nil
ab._hm = nil
ab._cache = {
    viewmodel = {},
    weapon_chams = {}
}

ab.setup = function(ac)
    ab._features = ac
end

ab._restore = function(ac)
    for ad, ae in ac do
        if ad:IsA("BasePart") or ad:IsA("MeshPart") then
            ad.Material = ae.Material
            ad.Color = ae.Color
            ad.Transparency = ae.Transparency
        elseif ad:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or ad:IsA("Texture") then
            ad.Parent = ae.Parent
        end
    end
    table.clear(ac)
end

ab._apply = function(ac, ad, ae, af)
    for ag, ah in ac do
        if ah then
            for ai, aj in ah:GetDescendants() do
                if aj:IsA("BasePart") or aj:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    if aj.Transparency ~= 1 and not (af and aj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")) then
                        if not ae[aj] then
                            ae[aj] = { Material = aj.Material, Color = aj.Color, Transparency = aj.Transparency }
                        end
                        aj.Material = Enum.Material[ad.material]
                        aj.Color = ad.color
                        aj.Transparency = ad.transparency
                    end
                elseif aj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or aj:IsA("Texture") then
                    if not ae[aj] then
                        ae[aj] = { Parent = aj.Parent }
                    end
                    aj.Parent = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

ab.load = function()
    ab._hm = aa.new()

    local ac = a.load('f')
    local ad = grab("BulletInterface").newBullet
    ab._hm:register_hook("custom_tracers", ad, function(ae, af)
        local ag = ab._features
        if ag and ag.visuals.tracers.enable and af.extra and af.extra.firearmObject then
            af.tracerless = true
            local ah = af.position
            local ai = ac._target or ah + af.velocity.Unit * 1000
            pcall(function()
                local aj = {}
                for ak, al in { ah, ai } do
                    local am = Instance.new("Part")
                    am.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
                    am.Transparency = 1
                    am.Anchored = true
                    am.CanCollide = false
                    am.CanTouch = false
                    am.CanQuery = false
                    am.CFrame = CFrame.new(al)
                    am.Parent = workspace
                    aj[ak] = am
                end
                local ak = Instance.new("Attachment")
                ak.Parent = aj[1]
                local al = Instance.new("Attachment")
                al.Parent = aj[2]
                local am = Instance.new("Beam")
                am.Texture = ag.textures[ag.visuals.tracers.texture] or "rbxassetid://7136858729"
                am.TextureLength = 1
                am.Color = ColorSequence.new(ag.visuals.tracers.color)
                am.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.1, 0.1)
                am.Attachment0 = ak
                am.Attachment1 = al
                am.LightEmission = 1
                am.LightInfluence = 0
                am.Width0 = ag.visuals.tracers.thickness
                am.Width1 = ag.visuals.tracers.thickness
                am.FaceCamera = true
                am.Parent = aj[1]
                task.spawn(function()
                    local an = tick()
                    while tick() - an < ag.visuals.tracers.lifetime do
                        am.Transparency = NumberSequence.new((tick() - an) / ag.visuals.tracers.lifetime)
                        task.wait()
                    end
                    am:Destroy()
                    aj[1]:Destroy()
                    aj[2]:Destroy()
                end)
            end)
        end
        return ae(af)
    end)

    ab._connection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not ab._features then return end
        local ae = grab("CharacterInterface").getCharacterObject()
        if not ae then return end

        if ab._features.visuals.viewmodel.enable then
            ab._apply({ae._leftArmModel, ae._rightArmModel}, ab._features.visuals.viewmodel, ab._cache.viewmodel)
        elseif next(ab._cache.viewmodel) then
            ab._restore(ab._cache.viewmodel)
        end

        if ab._features.visuals.weapon_chams.enable then
            local af = ae._weaponController and ae._weaponController:getActiveWeapon()
            local ag = af and af:getWeaponModel()
            if ag then
                ab._apply({ag}, ab._features.visuals.weapon_chams, ab._cache.weapon_chams, true)
            end
        elseif next(ab._cache.weapon_chams) then
            ab._restore(ab._cache.weapon_chams)
        end
    end)
end

ab.unload = function()
    if ab._connection then
        ab._connection:Disconnect()
        ab._connection = nil
    end
    if ab._hm then
        ab._hm:unregister_hook("custom_tracers")
    end
    ab._restore(ab._cache.viewmodel)
    ab._restore(ab._cache.weapon_chams)
end

return ab end function a.h()
local aa = a.load('c')
local ab = game:GetService("Lighting")
local ac = {}
ac._cache = {}
ac._hm = nil

ac.setup = function(ad)
    ac._hm = aa.new()
    ac._cache.fog_end = ab.FogEnd
    ac._cache.fog_start = ab.FogStart
    ac._cache.brightness = ab.Brightness
    ac._cache.ambient = ab.Ambient
    ac._cache.outdoor_ambient = ab.OutdoorAmbient
    ac._cache.clock_time = ab.ClockTime

    ac._cache.fov = grab("PlayerSettingsInterface").getValue("fov")
    local ae = ab:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
    ac._cache.sky_instance = ae
    ac._cache.sky = ae and {
        up = ae.SkyboxUp,
        down = ae.SkyboxDn,
        left = ae.SkyboxLf,
        right = ae.SkyboxRt,
        front = ae.SkyboxFt,
        back = ae.SkyboxBk,
    } or nil

    ac._connection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local af = grab("CharacterInterface").getCharacterObject()

        if ad.world.camera.custom_fov then
            grab("PlayerSettingsInterface").setValue("fov", ad.world.camera.fov_value)
            if af then
                af.unaimedfov = ad.world.camera.fov_value
            end
        else
            grab("PlayerSettingsInterface").setValue("fov", ac._cache.fov)
        end

        if not af then return end

        if ad.world.movement.infinite_stamina then
            af:setStamina(1)
        end

        local ag = af._weaponController:getActiveWeapon()
        af:setBaseWalkSpeed(ad.world.movement.speed_modifier and ad.world.movement.speed_value or (ag and ag:getWeaponStat("walkspeed")) or 14)

        if ad.world.movement.infinite_slide and af._inSlideLoop then
            af._slideSpeed = 30
        end

        if ad.world.movement.no_slide_cooldown then
            af.dived = false
        end

        if ad.world.movement.super_jump then
            af._humanoid.JumpPower = 70
        end

        if ad.world.movement.no_fall_damage then
            local ah = af._rootPart.Velocity
            if ah.Y < -55 then
                af._rootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(ah.X, -55, ah.Z)
            end
        end

        sethiddenproperty(workspace.Terrain, "Decoration", not ad.world.lighting.no_grass)

        if ad.world.lighting.no_fog then
            ab.FogEnd = 9999999
            ab.FogStart = 9999999
        else
            ab.FogEnd = ac._cache.fog_end
            ab.FogStart = ac._cache.fog_start
        end

        if ad.world.lighting.fullbright then
            ab.Brightness = 2
            ab.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
            ab.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
        elseif not ad.world.lighting.custom_ambient then
            ab.Brightness = ac._cache.brightness
            ab.Ambient = ac._cache.ambient
            ab.OutdoorAmbient = ac._cache.outdoor_ambient
        end

        if ad.world.lighting.custom_time then
            ab.ClockTime = ad.world.lighting.time_value
        else
            ab.ClockTime = ac._cache.clock_time
        end

        if ad.world.lighting.custom_ambient and not ad.world.lighting.fullbright then
            ab.Ambient = ad.world.lighting.ambient_color
            ab.OutdoorAmbient = ad.world.lighting.ambient_color
        end

        if ad.world.lighting.custom_sky then
            local ah = ac._cache.sky_instance or ab:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
            if not ah then
                ah = Instance.new("Sky")
                ah.Parent = ab
                ac._cache.sky_instance = ah
            end
            local ai = ad.skies[ad.world.lighting.sky]
            if ai then
                ah.SkyboxUp = ai.up
                ah.SkyboxDn = ai.dn
                ah.SkyboxLf = ai.lf
                ah.SkyboxRt = ai.rt
                ah.SkyboxFt = ai.ft
                ah.SkyboxBk = ai.bk
            end
        else
            local ah = ac._cache.sky and ab.Sky
            if ah then
                ah.SkyboxUp = ac._cache.sky.up
                ah.SkyboxDn = ac._cache.sky.down
                ah.SkyboxLf = ac._cache.sky.left
                ah.SkyboxRt = ac._cache.sky.right
                ah.SkyboxFt = ac._cache.sky.front
                ah.SkyboxBk = ac._cache.sky.back
            elseif ac._cache.sky_instance then
                ac._cache.sky_instance:Destroy()
                ac._cache.sky_instance = nil
            end
        end
    end)

    local af = grab("AudioSystem")
    local ag = af.playSoundId

    ac._hm:register_hook("sound_replace_1p", ag, function(ah, ai, ...)
        if ad.world.sounds.replace_gunshots then
            ai = ad.sounds[ad.world.sounds.gunshot_sound] or ai
        end
        return ah(ai, ...)
    end)

    local ah = af.playSound
    ac._hm:register_hook("sound_replace_hit", ah, function(ai, aj, ...)
        if ad.world.sounds.replace_hits and aj == "hitmarker" then
            local ak = ad.sounds[ad.world.sounds.hit_sound]
            if ak then
                return af.playSoundId(ak, 1, 1, 1.5, nil)
            end
        end
        return ai(aj, ...)
    end)

    for ai, aj in {
        {"unlock_all", grab("PlayerDataUtils").ownsWeapon},
        {"unlock_all_attachments", grab("PlayerDataUtils").ownsAttachment},
        {"unlock_all_skins", grab("SkinCaseUtils").ownsWeaponSkin},
    } do
        ac._hm:register_hook(aj[1], aj[2], function(ak, ...)
            if ad.world.exploits.unlock_all then
                return true
            end
            return ak(...)
        end)
    end

    local ai = grab("CharacterInterface").getTimeTillSpawn
    ac._hm:register_hook("instant_respawn", ai, function(aj, ...)
        if ad.world.exploits.instant_respawn then
            return 0
        end
        return aj(...)
    end)

    local aj = grab("PlayerDataUtils").getTagData
    ac._hm:register_hook("nametag_spoof", aj, function(ak, ...)
        local al = ak(...)
        if ad.world.exploits.nametag_spoof.enable and ad.world.exploits.nametag_spoof.text ~= "" then
            al.text = ad.world.exploits.nametag_spoof.text
        end
        return al
    end)

    local ak
    pcall(function()
        for al, am in debug.getupvalues(grab("NetworkClient").fireReady) do
            if type(am) == "table" and type(am.startvotekick) == "function" then
                ak = am
            end
        end
    end)

    if ak then
        ac._hm:register_hook("antikick", ak.startvotekick, function(al, am, ...)
            setstackhidden(al, true)
            local an = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
            if ad.world.exploits.antikick and (am == an.Name or am == an.DisplayName) then
                pcall(function()
                    game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, an)
                end)
            end
            return al(am, ...)
        end)
    else
        warn("antikick: votekick handler not found")
    end

    ac._cache.camo_db = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Content"):WaitForChild("ProductionContent"):WaitForChild("CamoDatabase"))
    local al = grab("PlayerDataUtils").getInventoryData
    ac._hm:register_hook("unlock_all_skins_inv", al, function(am, ...)
        local an = am(...)
        if ad.world.exploits.unlock_all then
            for ao, ap in ac._cache.camo_db do
                if ap.Case then
                    if not an[ap.Case] then
                        an[ap.Case] = { Skins = {} }
                    end
                    if not an[ap.Case].Skins[ao] then
                        an[ap.Case].Skins[ao] = { ALL = true }
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return an
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        local am = game:GetService("Players")
        repeat task.wait() until #am:GetPlayers() > 0
        local an = grab("ReplicationInterface").getEntry(am:GetPlayers()[1])
        local ao = an and an:getThirdPersonObject()
        if ao then
            ac._hm:register_hook("sound_replace_3p", ao.kickWeapon, function(ap, aq, ar, as, at, au)
                setstackhidden(ap, true)
                if ad.world.sounds.replace_gunshots then
                    as = ad.sounds[ad.world.sounds.gunshot_sound] or as
                end
                return ap(aq, ar, as, at, au)
            end)
        end
    end)
end

ac.unload = function()
    if ac._connection then
        ac._connection:Disconnect()
        ac._connection = nil
    end
    if ac._hm then
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("sound_replace_1p")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("sound_replace_3p")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("sound_replace_hit")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("unlock_all")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("unlock_all_attachments")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("unlock_all_skins")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("instant_respawn")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("nametag_spoof")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("antikick")
        ac._hm:unregister_hook("unlock_all_skins_inv")
    end
    local ad = grab("CharacterInterface").getCharacterObject()
    if ad then
        local ae = ad._weaponController:getActiveWeapon()
        ad:setBaseWalkSpeed(ae and ae:getWeaponStat("walkspeed") or 14)
    end
    grab("PlayerSettingsInterface").setValue("fov", ac._cache.fov)
    ab.FogEnd = ac._cache.fog_end
    ab.FogStart = ac._cache.fog_start
    ab.Brightness = ac._cache.brightness
    ab.Ambient = ac._cache.ambient
    ab.OutdoorAmbient = ac._cache.outdoor_ambient
    ab.ClockTime = ac._cache.clock_time
    if ac._cache.sky then
        local ae = ab.Sky
        if ae then
            ae.SkyboxUp = ac._cache.sky.up
            ae.SkyboxDn = ac._cache.sky.down
            ae.SkyboxLf = ac._cache.sky.left
            ae.SkyboxRt = ac._cache.sky.right
            ae.SkyboxFt = ac._cache.sky.front
            ae.SkyboxBk = ac._cache.sky.back
        end
    elseif ac._cache.sky_instance then
        ac._cache.sky_instance:Destroy()
        ac._cache.sky_instance = nil
    end
    sethiddenproperty(workspace.Terrain, "Decoration", true)
end

return ac end function a.i()
local aa = a.load('a')

return function(ab)
    local ac = aa.CreateWindow("getcelsius.net")
    ac:Watermark({ GameName = true, Players = true, FPS = true })

    local ad = function(ad, ae, af, ag, ah)
        return ad:Toggle(ae, {
            Text = af,
            Default = ag[ah],
            Callback = function(ai)
                ag[ah] = ai
            end,
        })
    end

    local ae = function(ae, af, ag, ah, ai)
        ae:AddColorpicker(af, {
            Default = ah[ai],
            Title = ag,
            Callback = function(aj)
                ah[ai] = aj
            end,
        })
    end

    local af = function(af, ag, ah, ai, aj, ak, al, am)
        af:AddSlider(ag, {
            Text = ah,
            Min = ak,
            Max = al,
            Default = ai[aj],
            Rounding = am,
            Callback = function(an)
                ai[aj] = an
            end,
        })
    end

    local ag = function(ag, ah, ai, aj, ak, al)
        ag:AddDropdown(ah, {
            Text = ai,
            Values = aj,
            Default = ak[al],
            Callback = function(am)
                ak[al] = am
            end,
        })
    end

    local ah = ac:Tab("Combat")
    local ai = ah:Section("Silent Aim", "left")

    ad(ai, "SilentAim", "Enable", ab.combat.silent_aim, "enable")
    ad(ai, "SA_VisibleCheck", "Visible Check", ab.combat.silent_aim, "visible_check")
    ad(ai, "SA_TeamCheck", "Team Check", ab.combat.silent_aim, "team_check")
    ae(ad(ai, "SA_FOV_Enable", "Show FOV", ab.combat.silent_aim.fov, "enable"),
        "SA_FOV_Color", "FOV Color", ab.combat.silent_aim.fov, "color")
    ae(ad(ai, "SA_TargetLine", "Target Line", ab.combat.silent_aim.target_line, "enable"),
        "SA_Line_Color", "Line Color", ab.combat.silent_aim.target_line, "color")
    ad(ai, "SA_Backtrack", "Backtrack", ab.combat.silent_aim.backtrack, "enable")
    ad(ai, "SA_MaxDist_Enable", "Max Distance", ab.combat.silent_aim.max_distance, "enable")
    ag(ai, "SA_Hitpart", "Hit Part", {"Head", "Torso", "Random"}, ab.combat.silent_aim, "hitpart")
    af(ai, "SA_FOV_Size", "FOV Size", ab.combat.silent_aim.fov, "size", 10, 500, 0)
    af(ai, "SA_FOV_Thickness", "FOV Thickness", ab.combat.silent_aim.fov, "thickness", 1, 5, 0)
    af(ai, "SA_LineWidth", "Line Width", ab.combat.silent_aim.target_line, "width", 1, 5, 0)
    af(ai, "SA_MaxDist_Range", "Max Distance Range", ab.combat.silent_aim.max_distance, "range", 50, 1000, 0)
    af(ai, "SA_Backtrack_Time", "Backtrack Time", ab.combat.silent_aim.backtrack, "time", 0, 500, 0)

    local aj = ah:Section("Gun Mods", "right")

    ad(aj, "GM_NoRecoil", "No Recoil", ab.combat.gun_mods, "no_recoil")
    ad(aj, "GM_NoSway", "No Sway", ab.combat.gun_mods, "no_sway")
    ad(aj, "GM_NoEquipTime", "No Equip Time", ab.combat.gun_mods, "no_equip_time")
    ad(aj, "GM_NoSuppression", "No Suppression", ab.combat.gun_mods, "no_suppression")
    ad(aj, "GM_NoCameraBob", "No Camera Bob", ab.combat.gun_mods, "no_camera_bob")
    ad(aj, "GM_NoSpread", "No Spread", ab.combat.gun_mods, "no_spread")
    ad(aj, "GM_RapidFire", "Rapid Fire", ab.combat.gun_mods, "rapid_fire")
    ad(aj, "GM_NoFlinch", "No Flinch / Damage Punch", ab.combat.gun_mods, "no_flinch")
    ad(aj, "GM_InstantReload", "Instant Reload", ab.combat.gun_mods, "instant_reload")
    ad(aj, "GM_NoRechamber", "No Bolt Re-chamber", ab.combat.gun_mods, "no_rechamber")
    ad(aj, "GM_ReloadCancel", "Reload Cancel Abuse", ab.combat.gun_mods, "reload_cancel")
    ad(aj, "GM_InstantADS", "Instant ADS", ab.combat.gun_mods, "instant_ads")

    local ak = ah:Section("Rage", "right")

    ad(ak, "Rage_Autoshoot", "Autoshoot", ab.combat.rage, "autoshoot")

    local al = ah:Section("Triggerbot", "right")

    ad(al, "Triggerbot_Enable", "Enable", ab.combat.triggerbot, "enable")
    ad(al, "Triggerbot_TeamCheck", "Team Check", ab.combat.triggerbot, "team_check")
    ad(al, "Triggerbot_VisibleCheck", "Visible Check", ab.combat.triggerbot, "visible_check")
    af(al, "Triggerbot_Delay", "Delay", ab.combat.triggerbot, "delay", 0, 1000, 1)

    local am = ah:Section("Aim Assist", "left")

    ad(am, "AA_Enable", "Enable", ab.combat.aim_assist, "enable")
    af(am, "AA_Strength", "Strength", ab.combat.aim_assist, "strength", 0.1, 5, 1)

    local an = ac:Tab("Visuals")
    local ao = an:Section("Players", "left")

    ad(ao, "PlayerESP", "Enable", ab.visuals.player_esp, "Enable")
    ad(ao, "PlayerESP_TeamCheck", "Team Check", ab.visuals.player_esp.TeamCheck, "Enable")
    ad(ao, "PlayerESP_VisibleCheck", "Visible Check", ab.visuals.player_esp.VisibleCheck, "Enable")
    ae(ad(ao, "PlayerESP_Name", "Name", ab.visuals.player_esp.Name, "Enable"),
        "PlayerESP_Name_Color", "Name Color", ab.visuals.player_esp.Name, "Color")
    ae(ad(ao, "PlayerESP_Distance", "Distance", ab.visuals.player_esp.Distance, "Enable"),
        "PlayerESP_Distance_Color", "Distance Color", ab.visuals.player_esp.Distance, "Color")
    ae(ad(ao, "PlayerESP_Skeleton", "Skeleton", ab.visuals.player_esp.Skeleton, "Enable"),
        "PlayerESP_Skeleton_Color", "Skeleton Color", ab.visuals.player_esp.Skeleton, "Color")
    ae(ad(ao, "PlayerESP_Weapon", "Weapon", ab.visuals.player_esp.Weapon, "Enable"),
        "PlayerESP_Weapon_Color", "Weapon Color", ab.visuals.player_esp.Weapon, "Color")
    ae(ad(ao, "PlayerESP_Box", "Box", ab.visuals.player_esp.Box, "Enable"),
        "PlayerESP_Box_Color", "Box Color", ab.visuals.player_esp.Box, "Color")
    ad(ao, "PlayerESP_HealthBar", "Health Bar", ab.visuals.player_esp.HealthBar, "Enable")
    ae(ad(ao, "PlayerESP_Chams", "Chams", ab.visuals.player_esp.Chams, "Enable"),
        "PlayerESP_Chams_Color", "Chams Color", ab.visuals.player_esp.Chams, "Color")
    ae(ad(ao, "PlayerESP_HealthText", "Health Text", ab.visuals.player_esp.HealthText, "Enable"),
        "PlayerESP_HealthText_Color", "Health Text Color", ab.visuals.player_esp.HealthText, "Color")
    ad(ao, "PlayerESP_MaxDist", "Max Distance", ab.visuals.player_esp.MaxDistance, "Enable")
    ag(ao, "PlayerESP_BoxMode", "Box Mode", {"Corner", "Full"}, ab.visuals.player_esp.Box, "Mode")
    af(ao, "PlayerESP_MaxDist_Range", "Max Distance", ab.visuals.player_esp.MaxDistance, "Range", 50, 2000, 0)
    af(ao, "PlayerESP_Skeleton_Thickness", "Skeleton Thickness", ab.visuals.player_esp.Skeleton, "Thickness", 1, 5, 1)

        end

    local s2  = type(getactorthreads) == "function" and type(run_on_thread) == "function"
    local s12 = type(getactors)       == "function" and type(run_on_actor)  == "function"
    if s2 then
        local t = getactorthreads(); t = t and t[1]
        if t then run_on_thread(t, s8) end
    elseif s12 then
        local a = getactors(); a = a and a[1]
        if a then run_on_actor(a, s8) end
    else
        local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        if lp then lp:Kick("Unsupported executor: missing getactorthreads/run_on_thread and getactors/run_on_actor")
        else error("Unsupported executor: missing getactorthreads/run_on_thread and getactors/run_on_actor", 0) end
    end
    return true
end

    warn("celsius crack env: " .. tostring(err))
